### [1] [Mutual Coupling and Visibility Effects in 2D Holographic Curvature-Reconfigurable Apertures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14504)
*Hang Lin,Shu Sun*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种二维全息曲率可重构孔径（HoloCuRA），通过几何模型和电磁耦合模型联合分析曲率、互耦和方向相关可见性对全息MIMO阵列性能的影响，发现互耦主要影响局部方向图波动，而可见性主导全局方向图和频谱效率变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息多输入多输出（MIMO）阵列虽能提供高空间分辨率和灵活的波前控制，但在曲面或共形部署时会同时引入曲率、互耦（MC）以及方向相关可见性三个关键问题。现有研究缺乏对这三种效应联合作用的系统分析，因此需要建立统一的分析框架来准确评估曲面全息MIMO阵列的电磁性能。

Method: 1. 建立基于几何的模型：表征单元位置、波束指向和空间相关性；2. 建立基于阻抗的耦合矩阵：捕获电磁互耦效应；3. 引入可见性向量：描述方向相关的阴影遮挡效应；4. 构建二维全息曲率可重构孔径（HoloCuRA）系统模型；5. 在不同曲率条件下评估相对方向图和频谱效率。

Result: 1. 互耦（MC）主要重塑局部方向图波动，对全局性能影响有限；2. 可见性效应主导全局方向图特性；3. 在各向同性散射信道和CDL-A信道下，可见性均是频谱效率变化的主导因素；4. 曲率变化会显著改变阵列的电磁特性，但不同物理机制的影响具有明显区分度。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了全息MIMO阵列在曲面部署时三种关键物理效应的不同作用机制：互耦主要影响局部特性，而可见性决定全局性能。这一发现为曲面全息MIMO阵列的设计与优化提供了重要理论指导，表明在实际工程中应重点关注可见性管理和方向相关阴影效应的抑制。

Abstract: Holographic multiple-input multiple-output arrays provide high spatial resolution and flexible wavefront control, but curved or conformal deployments jointly introduce curvature, mutual coupling (MC), and direction-dependent visibility. This paper studies a two-dimensional (2D) holographic curvaturereconfigurable aperture (HoloCuRA). A geometry-based model characterizes element positions, steering, and spatial correlation, while an impedance-based coupling matrix and a visibility vector capture electromagnetic interactions and shadowing. Relative directivity and spectral efficiency are evaluated versus curvature. Results show that MC mainly reshapes local directivity fluctuations, whereas visibility dominates the global pattern and spectral-efficiency variation under both isotropic scattering and CDL-A channels.

</details>


### [2] [UAV SAR Imaging via Diffusion Posterior Sampling under Non-Ideal Motion in ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14516)
*Keying Zhu,Le Liang,Jie Yang,Fan Liu,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机载合成孔径雷达(SAR)在低空通感一体化(ISAC)系统中的实际部署挑战，提出了一种基于扩散后验采样(DPS)的超分辨率SAR成像框架，能够处理非理想轨迹和未知位置误差，实现了高精度重建和显著的超分辨率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机载SAR在低空通感一体化系统中具有为地面用户提供通信服务同时感知周围环境的巨大潜力。然而，其实际部署面临两个关键挑战：(1)已知的非理想直线运动(ULM)轨迹，违反了传统SAR的基本假设；(2)未知的位置误差。这些因素会导致严重的图像散焦，制约了系统的实际应用性能。

Method: 1. 将ISAC SAR成像建模为精确线性逆问题，准确描述采用MIMO-OFDM波形的任意轨迹；2. 开发基于扩散后验采样(DPS)的超分辨率SAR框架，利用预训练扩散模型提供数据驱动的图像先验，同时通过精确物理模型在推理过程中强制测量一致性；3. 对推理过程进行加速以实现实际部署；4. 针对未知位置误差，开发联合迭代DPS与运动补偿(MoCo)优化算法，采用离散余弦变换重参数化和由粗到精的频谱调度策略。

Result: 1. 所提框架在多种轨迹下实现高精度重建，并保持显著的超分辨率能力，即使在半物理分辨率截断条件下峰值信噪比(PSNR)仍大于21.3 dB；2. 在未知运动误差条件下，联合DPS与MoCo算法达到23.65 dB的PSNR，残余位置误差估计误差仅为0.19 cm；3. 推理过程经过加速，满足实际实现需求。

Conclusion: 本文提出的DPS-based超分辨率SAR框架通过将数据驱动的扩散模型与精确物理模型相结合，有效解决了无人机载SAR在非理想轨迹和未知位置误差条件下的成像难题。该框架不仅实现了高质量的超分辨率重建，还通过联合优化策略实现了精确的运动补偿，为低空通感一体化系统的实际部署提供了有效的技术途径。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle-enabled synthetic aperture radar (SAR) offers significant potential for providing communication services to ground users while sensing the surrounding environment in low-altitude integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. However, its practical deployment is challenged by known non-ULM trajectories and unknown position errors, which violate conventional SAR assumptions and cause severe image defocusing. We address these non-ideal impacts by formulating the ISAC SAR imaging as an exact linear inverse problem to accurately model arbitrary trajectories with multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency division multiplexing waveforms. Based on this formulation, we develop a diffusion posterior sampling (DPS)-based super-resolution SAR framework, where a pre-trained diffusion model provides a data-driven image prior and the exact physical model enforces measurement consistency during posterior inference. The inference process is further accelerated for practical implementation. For unknown position errors, we develop a joint iterative DPS and motion compensation (MoCo) optimization algorithm with discrete cosine transform reparameterization and coarse-to-fine spectral scheduling. Simulations demonstrate that our framework achieves high-accuracy reconstruction across diverse trajectories and remarkable super-resolution, maintaining greater than 21.3 dB peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) even under half physical resolution truncation. Under unknown motion errors, the joint DPS and MoCo algorithm achieves 23.65 dB PSNR with a residual position-error estimation error of 0.19 cm.

</details>


### [3] [Performance Evaluation of Mobile Target Sensing and Tracking Across Multiple Sensing Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14528)
*Necati Kagan Erkek,Marco Di Renzo,Arman Shojaeifard,Kunjan Shah,Mohammad Heggo,Yasser Mestrah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 在PRS/OFDM ISAC系统中，通过目标指向波束成形、多参数融合估计与EKF跟踪，实现了移动扩展目标的高精度3D跟踪，显著优于全向和接收机指向波束成形方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在波束成形的PRS/OFDM ISAC（感知与通信一体化）框架下，研究移动扩展目标的感知与跟踪问题。针对MIMO散射信道中发射端-目标-接收端回波相干叠加的刚性散射体簇目标，需要解决目标感知与跟踪中的波束管理、参数估计与融合问题。

Method: 将目标建模为刚性散射体簇，其Tx-target-Rx回波在MIMO散射信道中相干叠加。采用PRS匹配滤波结合延迟质心算法估计时延，使用单簇2D-MUSIC算法估计方位角/俯仰角，采用基于FFT的多普勒估计方法；然后将这些估计结果用于独立定位和基于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）的跟踪。分别在单站和双站模式下，评估目标指向、接收机指向和全向波束成形三种波束成形策略。

Result: EKF跟踪相比独立定位将聚合3D RMSE从1.61 m降低至1.38 m。目标指向波束成形显著提升了跟踪精度，达到1.38 m RMSE，而全向和接收机指向波束成形分别为5.91 m和8.38 m。结果表明目标感知波束管理、时延-角度-多普勒融合以及感知几何构型对ISAC网络中移动目标跟踪具有重要影响。

Conclusion: 在PRS/OFDM ISAC框架中，目标指向波束成形结合EKF跟踪能够有效提升移动扩展目标的跟踪精度；时延-角度-多普勒参数融合与合理的感知几何构型是实现高精度移动目标跟踪的关键因素。

Abstract: Moving extended-target sensing and tracking are studied in a beamformed PRS/OFDM ISAC framework. The target is modeled as a rigid cluster of scatterers whose Tx--target--Rx echoes coherently combine in a MIMO scattering channel. Delay, azimuth/elevation, and Doppler are estimated using PRS matched filtering with delay centroiding, single-cluster 2D-MUSIC, and FFT-based Doppler estimation, then used for independent localization and EKF-based tracking. The framework is evaluated in monostatic and bistatic modes under target-directed, receiver-directed, and omnidirectional beamforming. Simulation results show that EKF tracking reduces the aggregate 3D RMSE from 1.61 m to 1.38 m relative to independent localization. Target-directed beamforming substantially improves tracking accuracy, achieving 1.38 m RMSE compared with 5.91 m and 8.38 m for omnidirectional and receiver-directed beamforming, respectively. The results highlight the importance of target-aware beam management, delay-angle-Doppler fusion, and sensing geometry for mobile-target tracking in ISAC networks.

</details>


### [4] [Efficient Multimodal Beam Prediction for Real-World 6G UAV Communications via Image-Position-Synthetic Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14593)
*Rifat Bin Reza,Md Ahadul Haque Suvo,Jia Uddin,Saifur Rahman Sabuj*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出分阶段多模态框架，融合视觉与位置衍生表征进行无人机毫米波波束预测，在真实数据集上验证了其互补性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于摄像头的波束选择可减少高移动性毫米波无人机链路中对穷举信道测量的需求，但视觉表征与位置衍生表征的互补价值及其在模态退化下的行为尚待深入研究。

Method: 本文提出了一种面向当前波束预测的分阶段多模态框架，采用真实世界无人机测量数据。所提出的32波束分类器结合了ImageNet预训练的EfficientNet-B3视觉编码器与两种确定性位置衍生表征：显式几何-运动学向量（Geometric-Kinematic）以及由位置构建的非线性合成特征基。融合策略采用图像与合成特征先进行早期融合，再与几何表征进行晚期融合。

Result: 在DeepSense 6G Scenario 23上的实验表明：图像-合成融合达到86.92% Top-1、99.03% Top-3和99.56% Top-5准确率，相比仅图像推理提升2.64个百分点，并优于仅合成推理的60.71% Top-1准确率；加入几何-运动学分支后获得最高性能，达到87.14% Top-1、99.12% Top-3和99.60% Top-5准确率。受控输入干预实验进一步验证了视觉退化下的互补行为以及对合成输入严重损坏的强敏感性。

Conclusion: 位置衍生的非线性特征为视觉上下文提供了互补信息，能够提升所评估的真实世界无人机场景中的当前波束预测性能。

Abstract: Camera-based beam selection can reduce the need for exhaustive channel measurement in highly mobile millimeter-wave UAV links, but the complementary value of visual and position-derived representations and their behavior under modality degradation require further investigation. This paper develops a staged multimodal framework for current-beam prediction using real-world UAV measurements. The proposed 32-beam classifier combines an ImageNet-pretrained EfficientNet-B3 visual encoder with two deterministic position-derived representations: an explicit Geometric-Kinematic vector and a nonlinear synthetic feature basis constructed from position. The image and synthetic representations are fused first, followed by late fusion with the geometry representation. Experiments on DeepSense 6G Scenario 23 show that Image-Synthetic fusion achieves 86.92% Top-1, 99.03% Top-3, and 99.56% Top-5 accuracy, improving Top-1 performance by 2.64 percentage points over image-only inference and outperforming the 60.71% Top-1 accuracy of synthetic-only inference. Adding the Geometric-Kinematic branch yields the highest measured performance, reaching 87.14% Top-1, 99.12% Top-3, and 99.60% Top-5 accuracy. Controlled input interventions further demonstrate complementary behavior under visual degradation and strong sensitivity to severe synthetic-input corruption. Position-derived nonlinear features provide complementary information to visual context and improve current-beam prediction in the evaluated real-world UAV setting.

</details>


### [5] [Adaptive DOA Estimation With KL-Based Kernel Bandwidth Adaptation Under Impulsive Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14623)
*Mojtaba Hajiabadi,Saeid Pakravan,Ghosheh Abed Hodtani,Ming Zeng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯核加权和KL散度在线带宽自适应的DOA估计方法，有效抑制脉冲噪声中的异常值，在低中等信噪比下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于高斯或二阶统计假设的DOA估计方法在脉冲噪声环境下性能显著下降，尤其对大幅值异常值敏感，因此需要开发能够自适应抑制异常值影响的DOA估计方法。

Method: 提出了一种自适应DOA估计器，采用高斯核加权来衰减大幅预测误差的影响；根据Kullback-Leibler (KL)散度准则在线调整核带宽；通过基于样本的KL准则近似实现递归带宽更新；空间滤波系数和核带宽均递归更新，DOA从最终的空间谱中恢复。

Result: 在高斯混合脉冲噪声下的仿真结果表明：系数自适应稳定，DOA估计精度优于代表性对比方法，在低中等信噪比条件下增益最显著；在线带宽自适应降低了对初始核带宽的敏感性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于KL散度准则在线调整核带宽的自适应DOA估计方法，能够有效抑制脉冲噪声中的异常值影响，在挑战性工作条件下实现了稳定且准确的DOA估计，且对初始参数选择具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation can degrade markedly in impulsive noise, where methods relying on Gaussian or second-order statistical assumptions are particularly susceptible to large-amplitude outliers. This paper develops an adaptive DOA estimator that employs Gaussian kernel weighting to attenuate the influence of large prediction errors while adjusting the kernel bandwidth online according to a Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence criterion. A sample-based approximation of the KL criterion yields a recursive bandwidth update that adapts the degree of outlier suppression to the observed error statistics rather than prescribing a fixed bandwidth a priori. The spatial-filter coefficients and kernel bandwidth are updated recursively, and the DOAs are recovered from the resulting spatial spectrum. Mean and mean-square analyses are developed under standard adaptive-filtering approximations to characterize the coefficient convergence and steady-state behavior. Simulation results under Gaussian-mixture impulsive noise demonstrate stable coefficient adaptation and improved DOA estimation accuracy over representative competing methods in several challenging operating regimes, with the most pronounced gains occurring at low-to-moderate signal-to-noise ratios. The results also indicate reduced sensitivity to the initial kernel bandwidth through online bandwidth adaptation.

</details>


### [6] [UL-Assisted Parametric CSI Feedback and Prediction for FDD Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14649)
*Hao Chang,Mingcheng Nie,Shuangyang Li,Chang Liu,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li,Wibowo Hardjawana*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对FDD系统CSI反馈过时问题，提出上行辅助的参数化反馈预测方案：UE直接编码下行导频观测上传，BS结合上行参数预测未来下行信道，经仿真验证优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在FDD系统中，上下行信道不完全互易，用户设备（UE）需要将下行信道状态信息（CSI）反馈给基站（BS）。然而，由于信道在CSI获取和利用之间会发生变化，反馈的CSI可能在BS使用前就已过时。因此，需要解决时变信道参数下CSI反馈的时效性问题。

Method: 本文提出了一种上行辅助的参数化CSI反馈与预测方案。具体步骤包括：（1）BS利用上下行信道参数的部分互易性，从UE发送的探测参考信号（SRS）中估计上行信道参数；（2）BS广播下行导频，使各UE获得关于其当前下行信道的观测；（3）各UE不直接估计下行信道，而是将观测直接编码为反馈符号并传输给BS；（4）BS解码器将反馈符号与部分互易的上行参数结合，预测未来的下行信道参数；（5）利用显式物理信道模型从预测的参数合成相应的下行信道矩阵。

Result: 在Sionna射线追踪（RT）数据集上的仿真结果表明，所提方案在不同信噪比和预测长度下，信道预测性能均优于所考虑的基准方案。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种利用上下行部分互易性的参数化CSI反馈与预测框架，通过UE直接编码观测而非显式估计信道，结合BS端的参数预测与物理信道模型合成，有效提升了FDD时变系统中下行信道预测的准确性和时效性。

Abstract: In frequency-division duplex (FDD) systems, the uplink (UL) and downlink (DL) channels are not fully reciprocal. Therefore, DL channel state information (CSI) estimated by each user equipment (UE) must be fed back to the base station (BS). However, because the channel varies between CSI acquisition and utilization, the feedback CSI may become outdated before it is used at the BS. To address this challenge, this paper proposes an UL-assisted parametric CSI feedback and prediction scheme for multi-user FDD systems with time-varying channel parameters. By exploiting the partial reciprocity between the UL and DL channel parameters, the BS first estimates the UL channel parameters from the sounding reference signals (SRSs) transmitted by the UEs. The BS then broadcasts DL pilots, allowing each UE to obtain observations containing information about its current DL channel. Instead of explicitly estimating the DL channel from these observations, each UE directly encodes them into feedback symbols and transmits the symbols to the BS. The BS decoder combines the feedback symbols with the partially reciprocal UL parameters to predict the future DL channel parameters. Finally, the corresponding DL channel matrix is synthesized from the predicted parameters using an explicit physical channel model. Simulation results on Sionna ray tracing (RT) dataset show that the proposed scheme outperforms the considered baselines in terms of channel prediction performance over different signal-to-noise ratios and prediction lengths.

</details>


### [7] [A QoE-Driven Framework for O-RAN Network Slicing Based on Empirical Multi-User VR Traffic Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14771)
*Ahmed Chyad,David Naseh,Maryam Hafeez,Syed Ali Raza Zaidi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于经验性多用户VR流量统计的QoE驱动网络切片框架，用于Open RAN中的动态资源分配。通过闭式n重卷积建模聚合数据速率和到达间隔时间，并基于实测数据验证。该框架通过近实时RIC xApp实现切片级PRB配额控制，在空口测试台上可从贪婪邻切片回收多达41%的PRB资源，同时保证VR切片QoE不降级。分析研究表明，固定速率合成负载会高估VR切片PRB占用68%，凸显了数据驱动切片维度设计的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR业务对QoE有严格要求，使得无线资源管理更加困难。静态切片分配造成资源浪费，而现有动态方法往往依赖缺乏经验基础的启发式惩罚函数，无法准确反映实际VR流量特征。

Method: 1) 基于经验性多用户VR流量统计数据进行资源分配决策；2) 使用闭式n重卷积模型建模n个并发用户的聚合数据速率和到达间隔时间，并通过实测数据验证（最多4个用户）；3) 将拟合的数据速率累积分布函数与实测吞吐量对比，生成基于百分位的满意度指标；4) 利用到达间隔规律设定切片目标；5) 通过近实时RIC xApp在E2SM-KPM和E2SM-RC接口上闭环控制，应用切片级物理资源块(PRB)配额；6) 进行解析分析估计不同用户数和信道质量下的PRB需求和单用户QoE。

Result: 1) 在空口测试台上，可从贪婪邻切片回收多达41%的小区承诺PRB，同时保证受保护VR切片不被降级；2) 解析研究给出了目标QoE无法达到的准入边界；3) 与固定速率合成负载的对照比较显示，后者会高估VR切片的PRB占用达68%，证明了数据驱动切片维度设计的必要性；4) 闭式n重卷积模型经验证适用于最多4个并发用户场景。

Conclusion: 基于经验性VR流量统计的数据驱动网络切片框架能够有效提升Open RAN资源利用效率，在保证VR业务QoE的同时显著减少资源浪费。相比固定速率合成负载方法，数据驱动的切片维度设计更加精确，为VR等QoE敏感业务的网络切片资源管理提供了实用的解决方案和理论分析工具。

Abstract: Virtual Reality (VR) requires strict Quality-of-Experience (QoE) guarantees, making radio resource management harder. Static slice allocations are wasteful, while dynamic approaches often rely on heuristic penalty functions lacking an empirical basis. We introduce a QoE-driven network-slicing framework for Open RAN in which resource allocation is guided by empirically derived multi-user VR traffic statistics. Closed-form n-fold convolutions model the aggregate data rate and inter-arrival time of n concurrent users, validated empirically for up to four. The fitted data-rate cumulative distribution function is checked against the measured throughput to give a percentile-based satisfaction indicator; the inter-arrival law sets the slice targets. A near-real-time RIC xApp closes a loop over the E2SM-KPM and E2SM-RC interfaces to apply slice-level Physical Resource Block (PRB) quotas. On an over-the-air testbed it reclaims up to 41% of the cell's committed PRBs from a greedy neighbouring slice while never reducing the protected VR slice. An analytical study then estimates the PRB demand and per-user QoE across user counts and channel qualities, giving the admission boundary beyond which the target QoE is unattainable. A controlled comparison further shows that a fixed-rate synthetic load overstates the VR slice's PRB footprint by 68%, showing the necessity of data-driven slice dimensioning.

</details>

Abstract: Multipath and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) propagation degrade global navigation satellite system (GNSS) positioning accuracy in urban environments. Although fisheye sky segmentation identifies obstructions along satellite directions, binary visibility does not fully characterize observation quality. This study proposes a point-supervised visual framework that predicts GNSS observation faults from fisheye images using supervision derived from GNSS observations. During offline labeling, a vision-assisted differential GNSS procedure selects multiple reference satellites based on sky visibility and elevation constraints. Double-difference pseudorange residuals automatically generate fault labels and sample importance weights at satellite projection locations. A residual-weighted loss is computed only at valid labeled points, enabling learning from sparse supervision without manual pixelwise fault annotations. The trained network generates dense fault probability maps, which are sampled at satellite projection locations to weight observations in weighted least squares positioning, reducing the influence of potentially faulty observations. The framework is evaluated using U-Net, DeepLabv3+, DINOv3, and SAM3 on urban vehicle data collected on a different day from the training data. All four models reduce horizontal root mean square error (RMSE) by 29.51% to 36.07% compared with SAM binary sky segmentation, with U-Net achieving the lowest RMSE of 0.78 m. The framework also provides positioning solutions during some intervals when the segmentation baseline fails. These results demonstrate the framework's generalization ability and show that GNSS-derived point supervision enables fisheye images to provide observation quality information beyond binary sky visibility, supporting GNSS fault detection.

Abstract: Oscillation monitors have root-mean-square (RMS) energy detectors at their core. However, this approach can yield poor detection in modern power systems with fast and largely unknown local dynamics. This letter proposes an algorithm to set frequency bands from the ambient noise spectrum to maximize the average detection performance.

Abstract: This paper proposes a channel-knowledge-map (CKM)-based predictive beamforming framework for integrated sensing, communication, and powering (ISCAP) of a mobile energy-constrained node in non-line-of-sight (NLoS) multipath environments. The CKM provides location-dependent channel information for both predictive beamforming and EKF-based tracking, while resolved path parameters estimated from the sensing echoes are used as observations. Based on the predicted node location, the transmit covariance and power-splitting ratio are jointly optimized to maximize wireless power-transfer efficiency subject to communication, sensing, transmit-power, and tracking-quality constraints. A finite-battery model and an adaptive inter-frame energy-management policy are further introduced to balance decoding energy consumption and battery preservation. The resulting non-convex fractional problem is solved using Dinkelbach's method, alternating optimization, and semidefinite relaxation. Numerical results show that the proposed framework maintains reliable tracking, satisfies the prescribed position-uncertainty requirement, achieves near-oracle wireless power-transfer performance after the initial tracking transient, and preserves more battery energy while meeting the required communication quality of service.

</details>

Result: 1. 推导得到了中断概率、遍历速率和吞吐量的高度精确表达式；2. 推导得到了检测概率、虚警概率和克拉美-罗下界的表达式；3. 揭示了高信噪比区域系统的分集阶数和编码增益等关键洞察；4. 数值结果验证了理论分析的正确性；5. 所提出的UAV-mounted HRIS辅助RSMA-ISAC系统相比传统多址接入方案和RIS系统具有显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: 本文提出的UAV-mounted HRIS辅助RSMA-ISAC方案，在考虑实际CSI和SIC不完美的情况下，通过理论分析和数值验证，证明了其在功率受限的ISAC场景中能够有效提升通信和感知性能，为下一代无线通信系统的设计提供了有价值的理论指导和技术方案。

Abstract: In integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems, strict sensing requirements can substantially constrain the transmit power available for communication. A hybrid reconfigurable intelligent surface (HRIS) with passive reflection and active amplification capabilities mounted on an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) can enhance coverage and strengthen communication links in such power-limited regimes. Meanwhile, rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) provides an effective transmission strategy for managing inter-user interference, particularly in dense multiuser wireless scenarios. Motivated by this, we analyze the performance of a UAV-mounted HRIS-assisted monostatic ISAC system employing RSMA scheme. We consider the imperfections in the channel state information and successive interference cancellation in the RSMA, which are inevitable in practical system implementations. We then derive highly accurate expressions for outage probability, ergodic rate, and throughput to evaluate communication performance over Nakagami-$m$ fading channels. These expressions reveal key insights into the diversity order and coding gain of the system in the high signal-to-noise ratio regime. To characterize the sensing performance, we derive expressions for the probability of detection, the probability of false alarm, and the Cramér-Rao lower bound. Numerical results validate the theoretical findings and highlight the performance gains of the proposed system over conventional multiple-access schemes and RIS-based systems.

</details>


### [12] [Physics-Informed Unsupervised Learning for Reliable Beamforming in Cluttered MIMO-ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15170)
*Yosefine Triwidyastuti,Nghia Thinh Nguyen,Tri Nhu Do*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息无监督学习（PI-UnSL）解决移动ISAC网络中受杂波、自干扰和过时CSI影响的联合波束成形与功率分配问题。通过嵌入闭合形式干扰统计和感知SINR解析下限，PI-UnSL单次前向传播即可覆盖整个速率-感知权衡曲线，构造性满足约束，并在问题间歇不可行时通过对偶校准保持稳定。数值表明其显著优于闭合形式基线和交替优化方法，且核心发现是物理知识而非网络架构使无标签学习成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文针对移动多用户MIMO集成感知与通信（ISAC）网络中的联合波束成形和功率分配问题。该场景存在杂波、残余自干扰和过时信道状态信息，且需满足每用户速率和感知信干噪比（SINR）约束。关键挑战在于：设计需每块重新计算，且远距离或弱目标可能导致感知约束无法满足，使问题间歇性不可行。

Method: 提出物理信息无监督学习（PI-UnSL）网络：1）通过构造精确满足功率和范数约束；2）基于闭合形式干扰统计条件化；3）单次前向传播覆盖整个权衡曲线；4）推导干扰感知合并器处的闭合形式感知SINR，得到必要的距离-雷达截面积-功率条件和与优先级无关的解析违反下限；5）证明双重目标低于可达下限时无固定点，并将无标签目标函数针对实测下限进行校准；6）利用物理知识而非网络架构本身实现无标签学习。

Result: 数值结果验证：1）确认了感知SINR下限和有界校准对偶；2）PI-UnSL显著提升了每实现联合服务质量，优于闭合形式基线；3）在匹配检测下，权衡曲线大部分区域PI-UnSL比时分ISAC提供更高速率；4）可靠性超过未校准交替优化求解器，且延迟仅为其一小部分；5）关键发现：物理知识而非网络架构使此类问题可无标签学习——五种架构从零开始均失败，但从闭合形式设计启动并以其干扰统计条件化时表现相当。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在具有杂波、残余自干扰和过时CSI的移动ISAC网络中，通过将物理知识（闭合形式干扰统计、解析违反下限）嵌入无监督学习框架，可以实现高效可靠的联合波束成形和功率分配。PI-UnSL方法通过构造满足约束、单次前向传播覆盖权衡曲线，并在问题间歇性不可行时通过校准对偶目标保证稳定性，显著优于传统闭合形式和交替优化方法。核心洞见是：对于此类具有复杂约束的物理层优化问题，嵌入物理知识比网络架构选择更为关键。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider joint beamforming and power allocation for a mobile multiuser MIMO integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) network with clutter, residual self-interference, and outdated channel state information, under per-user rate and sensing signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) constraints that must hold on every realization. The design must be recomputed every block, and distant or weak targets can make the sensing constraint unmeetable. We propose a physics-informed unsupervised learning (PI-UnSL) network that corrects the analytical design, meets the power and norm constraints exactly by construction, and is conditioned on closed-form interference statistics, spanning the whole tradeoff in one pass. We derive a closed-form sensing SINR at the interference-aware combiner, yielding a necessary range-radar-cross-section-power condition and a priority-independent analytic violation floor, so the problem is intermittently infeasible. We then show that a dual targeting below the attainable floor has no fixed point, and calibrate the label-free objective against the measured floor. Numerical results confirm the floor and the bounded calibrated dual, and show that PI-UnSL substantially raises per-realization joint quality of service over the closed-form baseline, delivers more rate than time-division ISAC at matched detection over most of the tradeoff, and exceeds the reliability of an uncalibrated alternating-optimization solver at a fraction of its latency. More broadly, the physical knowledge given to the network, not its architecture, makes such problems learnable without labels: five architectures fail alike from scratch and perform alike when started from the closed-form design and conditioned on its interference statistics.

</details>


### [13] [CSI-Aging-Aware Functional Split Optimization and KAN-LSTM Prediction for Open-RAN NTN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15178)
*Alexandar Tsvetanov Dimitrov,Yosefine Triwidyastuti,Eray Guven,Tri Nhu Do,Gunes Karabulut Kurt*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对LEO卫星Open RAN的功能拆分选择，建立精确星地几何模型揭示CSI新鲜度对拆分性能的决定性影响，推导出闭合形式最优拆分规则，并提出轻量级KAN-LSTM网络提前数秒预测拆分调度，实现接近最优的吞吐量性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文研究非地面网络（NTN）中开放无线接入网（Open RAN）的功能拆分选择问题。在低地球轨道（LEO）卫星承载射频单元的场景下，不同候选拆分方案（7.2x、6、2）将RAN置于透明到再生轴上的不同位置。由于星地链路存在时变特性（包括时延、多普勒频移等），拆分选择直接影响信道状态信息（CSI）的新鲜度，进而影响链路可靠性。现有方法多为被动响应式，无法满足拆分切换需要提前启动的实际需求。

Method: 1. 建立精确的星地几何模型：通过瞬时链路平面推导倾斜轨道和旋转地球条件下的闭合形式解析解（斜距、仰角、时延、多普勒）。
2. 信道建模：基于仰角驱动的视距/非视距（LOS/NLOS）混合信道推导接收信噪比（SNR）分布。
3. 拆分相关的CSI老化机制：建立各拆分方案的未编码误码率（BER）规律，量化地面闭环导致的馈线往返时延和老化引起的误码平台。
4. 优化问题建模：将拆分选择表述为基于可靠性、老化和前端容量约束的偏好集中式可行性问题，推导出四维状态坐标下的闭合形式区域规则。
5. 预测模型设计：提出紧凑的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）-LSTM混合神经网络，用于边缘xApp/rApp提前数秒预测拆分调度方案。

Result: 1. 解析解验证：推导的闭合形式几何解析解精确描述了星地链路特性。
2. 拆分特性揭示：不同拆分的本质区别在于CSI新鲜度，地面闭环方案因馈线往返时延存在老化导致的误码平台。
3. 优化求解：拆分选择问题的精确解为四维状态坐标下的闭合形式区域规则，与穷举枚举对比无一次失配。
4. 预测性能：KAN-LSTM预测器实现高调度精度，BER违规概率低于百分之一，可达到接近最优策略的吞吐量，优于朴素基线和被动响应式基线。

Conclusion: 本文针对LEO卫星Open RAN的功能拆分选择问题，建立了精确的星地几何与信道模型，揭示了CSI新鲜度是区分不同拆分方案的关键因素。通过推导出闭合形式的最优拆分区域规则，并进一步设计了轻量级的KAN-LSTM预测网络，实现了从被动响应到主动预测的转变。所提方案在保证可靠性的同时，以前瞻性调度满足了实际系统中拆分切换的时序要求，为NTN-Open RAN的边缘智能部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we study functional-split selection for an Open radio access network (RAN) deployed as a non-terrestrial network (NTN), in which a low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellite hosts the radio unit, so that each candidate split (7.2x, 6, 2) places the RAN at a distinct point of the transparent-to-regenerative axis. We capture the space-ground geometry exactly through an instantaneous link plane, which yields closed-form slant range, elevation, delay, and Doppler for an inclined orbit and a rotating Earth. On this geometry we derive the received-SNR density of the elevation-driven LOS/NLOS mixture channel and, through a split-dependent channel state information (CSI) aging mechanism, an uncoded bit-error-rate (BER) law for each split, establishing that what separates the splits is the freshness of the CSI each brings to its adaptation loops: a loop closed on the ground pays the feeder round-trip and incurs an aging-induced error floor. We formulate split selection as a prefer-centralized feasibility problem over reliability, aging, and fronthaul-capacity constraints, and we show that its exact solution is a closed-form region rule in four state coordinates, validated against exhaustive enumeration without a single mismatch. Because this solution is reactive whereas a split change must be initiated ahead of time, we propose a compact Kolmogorov-Arnold network (KAN)-LSTM, sized for an edge xApp/rApp, that predicts the split schedule seconds ahead; we show that it attains high schedule accuracy at a sub-percent BER-violation probability and delivers nearly all of the ground-truth policy's goodput, outperforming naive and reactive baselines.

</details>

*Saman Mostafavi,Robert W. Cox*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究负荷分解中不完全清单导致的归因模糊性问题，发现归因取决于目标-干扰路径的联合几何特性，而非仅由重构误差决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不完全的用电设备清单会在负荷分解中产生一种特定的归因模糊性：未被认领的用电需求可以在不改变总表重构的前提下，抵消已认领负荷的变化。作者希望研究允许的目标轨迹和干扰轨迹如何控制这种模糊性。

Method: 1. 使用有界有序马尔可夫链表示未被认领的需求（干扰项）；2. 采用无权接受器（unweighted acceptor）从目标中移除与委托不一致的路径，但不对幸存路径重新加权；3. 使用精确阶乘最大后验概率（exact factorial MAP）联合解码所有路径；4. 在两个全仪器化的商业建筑上进行验证，并设计合成实验确定边界条件。

Result: 1. 在两个全仪器化的商业建筑中，限制室外照明支持能改善屋顶压缩机数量的识别一致性，尽管没有压缩机链被直接约束；2. 这种增益依赖于干扰表示方式；3. 合成实验确定了边界条件：该效应在保持支持的变更下持续存在，但当真实路径违反委托支持或委托水平校准错误时会发生逆转；4. 归因取决于联合目标-干扰路径几何，而非单独的重构误差。

Conclusion: 负荷分解中的归因模糊性不能仅通过重构误差来理解，而是取决于目标轨迹和干扰轨迹的联合几何特性。适当的约束（如限制室外照明支持）可以通过改变路径空间的联合几何来改善对其他目标（如压缩机）的识别，但这种改善是有条件的，取决于干扰表示的准确性以及委托支持是否与实际一致。

Abstract: An incomplete inventory creates a specific attribution ambiguity in load disaggregation: unclaimed demand can offset changes in claimed loads without altering meter reconstruction. We study how the allowed target and nuisance trajectories govern this ambiguity. A bounded ordered Markov chain represents unclaimed demand, while an unweighted acceptor removes commissioning-inconsistent paths from one target without reweighting surviving paths; exact factorial MAP jointly decodes all paths. At two fully instrumented commercial buildings, restricting exterior-lighting support improves agreement on the count of rooftop compressors although no compressor chain is constrained; the gain depends on the nuisance representation. A synthetic experiment identifies the boundary: the effect persists under support-preserving changes but reverses when the true path violates commissioned support or the commissioned level is miscalibrated. Attribution therefore depends on joint target-nuisance path geometry, not reconstruction error alone.

</details>


### [15] [Structure-Exploiting ADMM for Low-Complexity Finite-Blocklength Beamforming in Cellular IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15300)
*Asim Ihsan,Muhammad Asif,Ali Arshad Nasir,Wali Ullah Khan,Galymzhan Nauryzbayev,Khaled M. Rabie*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构利用型共识ADMM框架，通过Kronecker结构分解和秩一信道投影，将大规模FBL感知波束赋形问题的计算复杂度大幅降低，在保持与通用求解器相近性能的同时实现4-6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超可靠低时延通信(URLLC)对任务关键型蜂窝物联网应用至关重要，需要可靠的短包传输和高效的无线资源分配。然而，随着用户数和天线数的增加，基于有限块长(FBL)的波束赋形计算复杂度急剧上升。现有逐次凸逼近(SCA)方法通常依赖通用凸优化求解器，未能充分利用所得波束赋形问题的特殊结构。

Method: 本文提出了一种利用结构特性的共识交替方向乘子法(ADMM)框架，用于下行多用户多输入单输出(MU-MISO)蜂窝物联网网络中的低复杂度FBL感知波束赋形。具体方法包括：(1) 在发射功率和单用户最小速率约束下最大化FBL和速率；(2) 每个SCA迭代产生一个凸二次约束二次规划(QCQP)，并将其重构为等价的共识问题；(3) 全局更新利用Kronecker结构将MN×MN的因子分解替换为共享的M×M因子分解；(4) 利用秩一信道矩阵实现低复杂度局部投影。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提方法在目标函数值、可行性和收敛性方面与CVX-SCA高度一致。在用户规模扩展情况下，所提方法相比CVX-SCA实现了超过4倍的端到端加速，相比标准ADMM-SCA实现了约6倍的每迭代加速，证明了其在蜂窝物联网资源分配中的计算效率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的结构利用型共识ADMM框架通过挖掘Kronecker结构和秩一信道矩阵特性，显著降低了FBL感知波束赋形的计算复杂度，为大规模蜂窝物联网网络中的实时资源分配提供了高效的优化解决方案。

Abstract: Ultra-reliable and low-latency communication (URLLC) is essential for mission-critical cellular Internet of Things (IoT) applications requiring reliable short-packet transmission and efficient radio-resource allocation. However, finite-blocklength (FBL)-aware beamforming becomes computationally demanding as the numbers of users and antennas increase. Existing successive convex approximation (SCA) methods typically rely on general-purpose convex solvers without exploiting the structure of the resulting beamforming problem. This paper develops a structure-exploiting consensus alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) framework for low-complexity FBL-aware beamforming in downlink multi-user multiple-input single-output (MU-MISO) cellular IoT networks. The FBL sum rate is maximized subject to transmit-power and per-user minimum-rate constraints. Each SCA iteration yields a convex quadratically constrained quadratic program (QCQP), reformulated into an equivalent consensus problem. For an M-antenna base station serving N IoT nodes, the global update exploits Kronecker structure to replace an MN-by-MN factorization with a shared M-by-M factorization, while rank-one channel matrices enable low-complexity local projections. CVX-SCA serves as a general-purpose benchmark, whereas standard ADMM-SCA isolates the gains from structural exploitation. Numerical results demonstrate close agreement with CVX-SCA in objective value, feasibility, and convergence. Under user scaling, the proposed method achieves more than 4-fold end-to-end speedup over CVX-SCA and approximately 6-fold per-iteration speedup over standard ADMM-SCA, demonstrating its computational efficiency for cellular IoT resource allocation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究连续孔径阵列（CAPA）通感一体化中的联合波束成形，发现即使照明算子无限秩，最优发射协方差仍保持有限秩；提出对偶松弛驱动的自适应模式富集算法，以远少于传统方法的电磁模式实现全空间最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续孔径阵列（CAPA）通过直接控制连续辐射孔径上的源电流分布，提供了一种新的电磁传输范式。本文研究面向空间扩展感知目标的CAPA使能的通感一体化（ISAC）。与点目标模型不同，扩展目标在目标区域上的电磁响应由孔径到区域算子表征，该照明算子可能具有无限秩，这排除了发射设计先验有限维简化的可能性。因此，需要在连续电磁域中重新思考联合通信和目标照明波束成形问题。

Result: 1. 理论上证明了最优发射协方差算子的有限秩结构，即使面对无限秩的照明算子；2. 建立了算子SDR的紧性理论保证；3. 证明了模式富集序列全局收敛到全空间最优解；4. 数值结果验证了有限秩最优结构，并表明所提方法相比截断SVD和空间傅里叶表示，能够以显著更少的电磁模式逼近全空间最优解。

Conclusion: 本文针对CAPA使能的通感一体化系统，在连续电磁域中建立了联合波束成形理论框架。核心贡献在于揭示了最优发射协方差算子的有限秩结构这一关键理论结果，克服了无限秩照明算子带来的维数灾难问题。所提出的对偶松弛驱动自适应模式富集方法能够系统地发现支撑最优解的有限电磁子空间，具有全局收敛保证和可计算的最优性证书，为连续孔径阵列的实际通感一体化设计提供了高效的计算途径。

Abstract: Continuous-aperture arrays (CAPAs) provide a new electromagnetic transmission paradigm by directly controlling the source-current distribution over a continuous radiating aperture. This paper studies CAPA-enabled integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) with spatially extended sensing targets, for which the electromagnetic response over each target region is characterized by an aperture-to-region operator. Unlike point-target models, the resulting illumination operator may have infinite rank, precluding a priori finite-dimensional reduction of the transmit design. We formulate the joint communication and target-illumination beamforming problem in the continuous electromagnetic domain and develop an operator semidefinite relaxation (SDR). A key structural result is that the optimal transmit covariance operators remain finite rank even when the aggregate illumination operator has infinite rank. We further establish the tightness of the operator SDR and provide a constructive rank-one recovery of the information beamformers. Since the finite electromagnetic subspace supporting the optimum is generally unknown, we develop a dual-slack-driven adaptive mode-enrichment method that discovers the required modes from the full-space optimality conditions. The dual-slack violation simultaneously identifies missing modes and yields a computable certificate of the remaining full-space optimality gap. Global convergence of the enrichment sequence to the full-space optimum is established. Numerical results validate the finite-rank optimal structure and show that the proposed method approaches the full-space optimum with substantially fewer electromagnetic modes than truncated-SVD and spatial-Fourier representations.

</details>

*Guanping Shang,Anzheng Tang,Kwok Kit Chan,Shenghui Song*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种OFDM-ISAC多模态语义感知框架，通过无线电编码器和变分无线电-视觉基础(VRVG)实现语义一致的无线电-视觉融合，实验证明其在用户匹配、视觉定位和物理参数估计方面的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义感知将综合感知与通信(ISAC)从物理参数估计扩展到利用无线电回波的任务导向表示，但现有多模态感知仍主要局限于视觉辅助的无线电物理参数估计，或通常将视觉和无线电模态分开处理，导致无线电表示未被充分利用于视觉任务增强。特征级融合能实现更紧密的跨模态交互，但回波干扰使学习用户特定的无线电表示及其与视觉实体的语义一致性变得复杂。

Method: 本文提出了一种用于正交频分复用(OFDM)-ISAC系统的多模态语义感知框架。具体包括：(1)提出无线电编码器，从叠加的导频回波中学习面向视觉定位的无线电表示；(2)提出变分无线电-视觉基础(VRVG)方法，促进学习的无线电表示与视觉空间特征之间的语义一致性；(3)将无线电表示构建为语义探针，通过跨模态融合突出视觉场景中的对应实体。

Conclusion: 研究结果证实了无线电表示学习和语义一致融合对多模态语义感知的益处，展示了通过跨模态语义一致性促进无线电表示在视觉任务中的有效利用的可行性。

Abstract: Semantic sensing extends integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) beyond physical parameter estimation by exploiting task-oriented representations from radio echoes. Nevertheless, existing multimodal sensing remains largely limited to vision-assisted radio physical parameter estimation or typically processes the visual and radio modalities separately, leaving radio representations underutilized for visual task enhancement. Feature-level fusion enables closer cross-modal interaction, while echo interference complicates the learning of user-specific radio representations and its semantic consistency with the visual entities. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a multimodal semantic sensing framework for orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM)-ISAC systems. We propose a radio encoder to learn radio representations oriented toward visual localization from superimposed pilot echoes, together with Variational Radio-Visual Grounding (VRVG) to promote semantic consistency between the learned radio representations and visual spatial features. The radio representations are then formulated as semantic probes to highlight the corresponding entities in the visual scene through cross-modal fusion. Experiments using real-world RCooper dataset show that the resulting synergistic radio-visual embeddings enable accurate radio-visual user matching and significantly improve both user-specific visual localization and physical parameter estimation performance. The results substantiate the benefits of radio representation learning and semantically consistent fusion for multimodal semantic sensing.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在10-180 kHz分析频段内，中值重复帧信噪比为48 dB，采集速率约为每频率0.18帧/秒（每次扫描约60秒），跨会话基线漂移约6%。对于ABS包含物，留一会话匹配在160 kHz时30次运行中成功定位29次（频段内87-97%，随机概率33%）。对于透析袋，所有36次非零对比运行均正确识别极性。有符号电压投影与圆柱对比因子相关（各运行间R²=0.68；聚合拟合R²=0.89）。浓度排序准确（Spearman ρ=0.85），尽管约10倍以上对比度时出现饱和。袋位置精度从最高对比度的6/6到匹配电导率时的2/6不等。匹配电导率袋产生约1.7% RMS扰动，而重复帧变异仅为0.4-0.6%。

Conclusion: 基于微控制器的多频EIT系统能够通过直接边界电压分析支持选定的检测和识别任务，无需复杂的图像重建。该系统为肌肉电化学等生物医学应用的未来研究提供了可行的低成本解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-frequency electrical impedance tomography (EIT) often requires specialized hardware. This challenge is especially pronounced in biomedical applications. We developed a 16-electrode EIT system using an Arduino GIGA R1 WiFi for generation and acquisition, with a discrete current source, three analog multiplexers, and basic signal conditioning. Dual cores separate waveform timing from switching and acquisition. A 3D-printed tank with silver-wire electrodes was tested at 11 frequencies (1-180 kHz). Across the 10-180 kHz analysis band, median repeat-frame signal-to-noise ratio was 48 dB, acquisition rate was ~0.18 frames/s per frequency (~60 s per sweep), and cross-session baseline drift was ~6%.
  KCl phantoms contained an insulating ABS cylinder or KCl-filled dialysis bags (0.001-1.0 M) spanning positive and negative conductivity contrasts. Boundary voltages were analyzed without reconstruction to estimate inclusion position, contrast polarity, and relative magnitude. Leave-one-session-out matching located ABS inclusions in 29/30 runs at 160 kHz (87-97% across the band; chance 33%). For dialysis bags, polarity was correctly identified in all 36 nonzero-contrast runs. Signed voltage projections correlated with the cylindrical contrast factor (R^2=0.68 across runs; R^2=0.89 for the aggregate fit). Concentration was ordered (Spearman rho=0.85), despite saturation above ~10-fold contrast. Bag position accuracy ranged from 6/6 at the highest contrasts to 2/6 at matched conductivity. The matched-conductivity bag produced a ~1.7% RMS perturbation against 0.4-0.6% repeat-frame variability.
  These results demonstrate that microcontroller-based multi-frequency EIT can support selected detection and identification tasks through direct boundary-voltage analysis, supporting future studies of muscle electrochemistry.

</details>


### [19] [Eunoia: Toward Application-Transparent, Scalable GPU Sharing in AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15404)
*Zongshen Wu,Marko Milenković,Jiarong Xing,Rahman Doost-Mohammady*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出Eunoia，一种动态实时AI-RAN GPU调度器，在无需修改AI应用和RAN负载的前提下，以RAN时隙粒度实现GPU资源共享，在保证5G RAN低延迟的同时提升AI性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-RAN架构通过将AI和RAN工作负载共置于同一商用硬件上，使下一代蜂窝网络更加灵活。然而，通用并发AI应用会在GPU计算资源上对延迟敏感的RAN产生干扰，因此需要一种能够协调AI与RAN共享GPU资源的调度机制。

Method: 作者提出Eunoia，一种动态实时AI-RAN GPU调度器。其核心特点包括：（1）对AI应用和RAN工作负载完全透明；（2）以RAN时隙（slot）为粒度进行调度；（3）部署于NVIDIA Aerial AI-RAN平台。

Result: 在27小区的AI-RAN环境中，Eunoia将RAN上行链路P99延迟控制在3毫秒以内，同时其AI性能优于其他RAN安全的静态分区方法。该结果在真实空口5G专网和仿真多小区网络中均得到验证。

Conclusion: Eunoia通过细粒度、透明的动态GPU调度，有效解决了AI-RAN场景下AI与RAN共存时的资源争用问题，在保证RAN严格时延要求的前提下提升了AI任务性能，为下一代AI-RAN系统的实用化部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The AI-RAN paradigm makes the next-generation cellular network more versatile by co-locating AI and RAN workloads on the same commodity hardware. Furthermore, the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) is derived to provide a theoretical benchmark for the proposed multi-view direction-sensing system. Abstract: In multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (MIMO-OFDM) systems, conventional receivers based on linear channel estimation and subband-averaged interference covariance reconstruction suffer serious performance degradation under strong interference. To address this issue, we propose a probabilistic framework for joint channel estimation and interference suppression using score-based prior learning. Specifically, we formulate a variational inference problem that exploits the bilinear structure of the interference by explicitly separating the interference channels from the interference symbols. To solve the resulting highly nonconvex bilinear inference problem, we develop a score-based variational inference algorithm that combines score-based turbo message passing for target-channel estimation with a reverse mean propagation framework for interference-channel inference, while the interference symbols are updated via linear minimum mean square error (LMMSE) estimation. We further design an interference rejection combining (IRC) detector with subcarrier-level covariance reconstruction based on the estimated interference channels, thereby avoiding the performance loss caused by conventional subband averaging. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm is able to effectively suppress the interference in channel estimation, as well as to accurately reconstruct the interference subspace for interference rejection in data detection, and thus significantly outperforms existing baselines.

Abstract: Radio frequency fingerprinting aims to identify transmitters by exploiting small differences in the emitted signal waveform. Common approaches exploit hardware-dependent imperfections for that purpose. This work instead investigates whether differences between digital signal processing implementations of a transmitter can be identified from their radiated signals. As a practical case study, five different software-based DSP implementations for generating a 2-FSK RTTY communication waveform are considered. In a monitoring receiver, a classifier can identify these DSP implementations using convolutional neural networks that are trained on augmented software-generated signals represented as raw IQ, power spectrum or instantaneous frequency. The trained networks are evaluated on over-the-air recordings obtained using a shortwave transceiver and a software-defined radio receiver. The best neural network combines IQ and the power spectrum representation and achieves an accuracy of up to 90%. The study further examines the influence of the signal SNR and the observation bandwidth on the classification accuracy. The results demonstrate that DSP implementations leave distinguishable characteristics in the transmitted signals and suggest that DSP-dependent fingerprinting can provide complementary information for specific emitter identification systems.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合近远场环境下可移动天线辅助通感一体化的物理层安全性能，提出了统一阵列响应模型和基于投影梯度上升的双层优化算法，证明混合场模型可达到近似全近场的保密速率性能，且远优于纯远场模型；同时发现模型失配会导致MA增益损失甚至劣于固定天线系统。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可移动天线(MA)能够增强通感一体化(ISAC)的物理层安全(PLS)性能，但现有工作大多假设通信用户(CU)和目标处于纯近场(NF)或纯远场(FF)传播模型。本文针对更实际的场景：由于MA天线位置更新导致传播场变化，多个CU、窃听者(Eve)和多个目标处于混合近远场环境中。

Result: 数值结果表明：采用混合场模型的MA-ISAC在SSR方面与采用全NF模型的PLS性能几乎相同，且两者均优于纯FF模型。在天线位置更新过程中使用不匹配的FF模型会导致MA增益损失，甚至可能使MA系统性能劣于使用正确模型的固定天线(FA)系统。

Conclusion: 本文提出了混合近远场环境下MA辅助ISAC的统一建模框架和高效优化算法，证明了混合场模型能够以接近全近场模型的精度实现优异的物理层安全性能，同时揭示了模型失配(特别是错误使用远场模型)对MA系统性能的严重负面影响，为MA辅助ISAC系统的实际部署提供了重要的理论指导和设计依据。

Abstract: Movable antenna (MA) can enhance the physical layer security (PLS) performance of integrated sensing and communications (ISAC). However, most existing works assume either near-field (NF) or far-field (FF) propagation models for both communication users (CUs) and targets. This paper considers a more practical scenario where multiple CUs, an eavesdropper (Eve), and multiple targets are in a mixed near- and far-field environment due to the changes in the propagation field caused by antenna position updates in MA with a unified array response and continuous NF/FF weights. To evaluate the PLS performance of MA-aided ISAC in mixed-field environments, we formulate an optimization problem to maximize the sum secrecy rate (SSR) of CUs by jointly designing the transmit beamforming and antenna positions at the transmitter. The problem is highly non-convex, so a two-layer algorithm is proposed to iteratively solve the problem using the projected gradient ascent (PGA) method and the Lagrangian dual transform. Since the abrupt switch between the NF and the FF models during antenna movement can cause the objective function to oscillate during iteration, the unified array response is combined with the Barzilai-Borwein step, Armijo line search, and precise projection to ensure that the SSR does not decrease during the iteration and the objective function is differentiable on the Rayleigh boundary. Numerical results show that the MA-ISAC using the mixed-field model achieves almost identical PLS performance to that of using the full NF model in terms of SSR, and both outperform the FF model. Using the mismatched FF model during antenna position updates results in a loss of MA gain, potentially making MA inferior to the FA-based system using the correct model.

</details>

Method: 本文构建了包含一个发射机、多个分布式接收机和一个未知位置速度的单目标的感知模型。针对纯多普勒感知和时延-多普勒感知两种模式，推导了Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）和位置等价FIM（EFIM）。进一步研究了混合接收机架构（仅部分接收机提供时延估计）的情况，并通过Wi-Fi带宽条件下的仿真实验进行验证。

Result: 可观测性分析表明：纯多普勒定位通常需要6个接收机才能实现目标定位；而时延-多普勒定位在非退化几何条件下仅需3个接收机即可实现局部可识别。仿真结果量化了带宽、多普勒噪声、接收机几何构型以及时延能力接收机可用性对定位性能的影响，证实了从纯多普勒感知到时延-多普勒感知的性能过渡。

Conclusion: 双基地时延信息显著提升了分布式Wi-Fi感知中三维运动目标的可观测性和定位性能。时延-多普勒联合感知大幅降低了对接收机数量的需求（从6个降至3个），为实际Wi-Fi感知系统的设计提供了理论依据，特别是在接收机资源受限的场景下，混合接收机架构具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates how bistatic delay information improves the observability and localization performance of 3D moving targets in distributed Wi-Fi sensing. Motivated by emerging IEEE 802.11bf WLAN sensing, we consider one transmitter, multiple distributed receivers, and a single target with unknown position and velocity. We compare Doppler-only sensing with delay-Doppler sensing, in which delay-capable receivers provide additional bistatic path-length information for target localization. We derive the Fisher information matrix (FIM) and the position equivalent FIM (EFIM) for both sensing formulations. The resulting observability analysis shows that Doppler-only localization generically requires six receivers, whereas delay-Doppler localization can become locally identifiable with three receivers under non-degenerate geometry. We further study mixed receiver architectures in which only a subset of receivers provides delay estimates. Simulations with Wi-Fi-like bandwidths confirm the transition and quantify the roles of bandwidth, Doppler noise, receiver geometry, and delay-capable receiver availability.

</details>


### [25] [Theoretical Investigation of Nonclassical Directional Emission in 1D and 2D Quantum-Dot Antenna Arrays and Quantum DoA Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15652)
*Angeru Pavan Kumar,Sandip Ghosal*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 纠正了量子点阵列辐射不对称性被误读为互易性破缺的观点，证明一阶相干性具有经典等价性，提出真正的量子特征在于二阶光子关联（光子反聚束），并设计了"量子天线证书"（不对称性+反聚束）来标识量子天线；同时完善了阵列量子光学的多个理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点阵列在定时Dicke态下表现出定向辐射不对称性，这一现象常被解释为通过量子态制备实现互易性破缺的证据。然而，作者指出这种解释存在问题：互易性关注的是发射-接收响应，而非辐射图样的对称性。此外，任何单激发态的一阶相干性都与经典相控阵列的功率图样完全等价，使得观测到的不对称性可以通过经典方式重现。因此，需要寻找真正的量子特征来区分量子天线与经典天线。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与数值验证相结合的方法。首先，严格区分互易性与辐射图样对称性的概念；其次，证明单激发态的一阶相干性与经典相控阵列的等价性；然后，分析二阶光子关联特性，发现单激发态在光子符合计数上违反经典下界；进而提出结合非零定向不对称性与光束峰值处光子反聚束的"量子天线证书"；此外，推导了紧致的不对称性边界、建立了平面阵列的正确镜像操作、修正了包含偶极-偶极耦合的集体衰减率，并导出了测向精度的饱和定律。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 澄清了定时Dicke态辐射不对称性不能作为互易性破缺的证据；(2) 证明了单激发态的一阶相干性与经典相控阵列完全等价，观测到的辐射不对称性可被经典重现；(3) 发现真正的量子特征存在于二阶关联中——单激发态违反光子符合计数的经典下界；(4) 提出了量子天线证书：非零定向不对称性+光束峰值处的光子反聚束；(5) 推导了紧致的不对称性边界；(6) 建立了平面阵列的正确镜像操作；(7) 修正了含偶极-偶极耦合的集体衰减率；(8) 导出了测向精度的饱和定律；(9) 所有理论结果均通过独立的数值实现进行了验证。

Conclusion: 本文纠正了量子点阵列辐射不对称性解释中的概念误区，明确指出辐射图样不对称性不等于互易性破缺，且一阶相干性具有经典等价性。真正的量子天线特征应通过二阶光子关联（特别是光子反聚束）来识别。作者提出的"量子天线证书"（定向不对称性+光子反聚束）为实验上验证量子天线的量子本质提供了可操作的标准。同时，本文在理论层面完善了阵列量子光学的多个基础问题，包括不对称性边界、镜像操作、集体衰减率修正和测向精度极限等。

Abstract: A quantum-dot array prepared in a timed Dicke state exhibits directional radiation asymmetry, which has often been interpreted as evidence of reciprocity breaking through quantum-state preparation. We show that this interpretation is incorrect: reciprocity concerns transmit-receive response, not necessarily pattern symmetry. Moreover, the first-order coherence of any single-excitation state is exactly equivalent to the power pattern of a classical phased array driven by conjugate weights, making the observed asymmetry classically reproducible. The genuine quantum signature instead appears in second-order correlations, where a single-excitation state violates the classical lower bound on photon coincidences. We therefore propose a quantum-antenna certificate combining nonzero directional asymmetry with photon antibunching at the beam peak. We further derive a tight asymmetry bound, establish the correct mirror operation for planar arrays, correct the collective decay rate with dipole-dipole coupling, and derive a saturation law for direction-finding precision. All theoretical results are validated through independent numerical implementations.

</details>

Abstract: Radio map estimation (RME), also known as spectrum cartography, aims to recover radio signal strength across multiple domains (e.g., spatial and spectral domains) from sparse measurements, naturally leading to a tensor completion problem. Recent advances show that deep learning-based generative models can provide data-driven structural constraints for RME, yielding substantial performance improvements over traditional model-based approaches. Diffusion models (DMs) represent the current state of the art in generative modeling and hold strong promise for advancing RME. However, existing diffusion-based RME methods often require extensive training data, necessitate retraining when scenarios or measurement formats change, and lack recoverability guarantees. This paper proposes a latent factor diffusion framework for spatio-spectral radio map tensor completion. By training two independent DMs on single-emitter's spatial loss field and spectral signature and embedding them into the factors of the radio map tensor, the proposed approach enables diffusion posterior sampling-based recovery with reduced training complexity as well as enhanced measurement format/sensing pattern flexibility. Recoverability and sample complexity guarantees under the proposed approach are also established. Numerical experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed method, showing strong generalization across city environments unseen in training data.

In a controlled two-probe experiment, the phantom was introduced and removed while the probe arrangement and measurement conditions were retained. This comparison was repeated under the same retained arrangement, with the same general $S_{21}$ differential behavior observed across repetitions. Near 6.3~GHz, measured $S_{21}$ was approximately $-25$~dB with the phantom present and approximately $-40$~dB in air, corresponding to an $\approx 16~\mathrm dB$. \textit{Ex vivo} goat brain measurements repeated more than five times showed reproducible behavior. Across 5.78--6.86~GHz, the \textit{ex vivo} goat-brain $S_{21}$ response closely followed that of the phantom, with an approximately figure-derived maximum operating-band difference of about 1.5~dB. The observed increase in $|S_{21}|$ is interpreted as a property of the complete probe--medium--probe system, including loading, mutual coupling, interfaces, geometry, propagation, and material loss. The measurement does not uniquely identify a tissue-intrinsic mechanism.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出一种共存框架：利用延迟-多普勒(DD)域中专门设计的扩频矩阵处理OTFS数据，该矩阵用于建立OTFS与OFDM波形之间的正交性，使两者能够在相同的时间-频率(TF)资源内共存并有效降低交叉干扰。

Conclusion: 所提出的共存框架成功实现了OFDM与OTFS的高效兼容共存，在保证后向兼容的同时具备前向扩展能力，为6G网络中多波形协同传输提供了有效的技术途径，在通信性能和感知性能方面均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: The upcoming sixth generation (6G) wireless networks are designed to support advanced applications that the current air interface based on orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) cannot accommodate, necessitating innovations in waveform design. Orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) has emerged as a promising alternative, offering enhanced performance particularly in high-mobility channel environments. However, deploying fundamentally different waveforms over the same frequencies fails to exploit the mutual relationships between them. In this paper, a forward-compatible yet backward-compatible coexistence framework is proposed, where various OFDM waveform options are incorporated while seamlessly integrating OTFS within the same network. The proposed coexistence paradigm involves utilizing a specially designed spreading matrix in the delay-Doppler (DD) domain to process OTFS data. The matrix is tailored to establish orthogonality between OTFS and OFDM waveforms, effectively reducing cross-interference when both coexist within the same time-frequency (TF) resources. Our results demonstrate up to a 6 dB improvement in bit error rate (BER) performance for the OTFS waveform compared to other coexistence schemes. The proposed approach also shows greater interference resilience and approaches the upper bounds of achievable rates. By utilizing the full bandwidth and time duration, it enhances range and velocity resolutions, leading to improved sensing performance. These findings are supported by mathematical analysis and extensive simulations for uplink scenarios.

</details>

Conclusion: 基于电磁信道模型，本文通过分析波数域中的块稀疏特性，并结合空间非平稳性的可见性因子建模，提出的可见性感知块稀疏贝叶斯算法有效解决了近场大规模MIMO系统中导频受限的非平稳稀疏信道估计问题，为未来近场通信系统的信道估计提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: This paper studies near-field massive MIMO channel estimation based on an electromagnetic channel model with limited pilots. Starting from Maxwell's equations, we derive the homogeneous background response and the scattering response in the spatial and wavenumber domains. It is found that a compact scattering cluster produces a localized receive--transmit transverse-wavenumber block across finite apertures, thereby yields a block-sparse NLOS channel coefficient matrix in the wavenumber domain. Considering the spatial non-stationarity of large-scale antenna arrays, we introduce visibility factors at the transmit and receive sides of the channel model. To solve the non-stationary sparse channel estimation problem in the wavenumber domain, we propose a visibility-aware block-sparse Bayesian channel estimation algorithm. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves lower channel-estimation NMSE than benchmark schemes.

Result: 1) 理论分析表明，在奇异态附近，与奇异性相关的分量对环境扰动更加敏感；2) 数值仿真验证了估计的极点频率具有数值稳定性，并展示了极点相关信道分量的环境敏感性；3) 所提出的功率分配方法将加权奇异信道暴露降低90.32%，同时保持传统水填充算法95%的名义速率。

Abstract: Electromagnetic information theory (EIT) brings electromagnetic physics into wireless channel analysis and offers a physical view of channel structures hidden by conventional models. This paper studies channel instability near electromag- netic singular states, where small environmental perturbations can cause large variations in the end-to-end channel. Using resolvent-based spectral analysis, we develop an electromag- netic multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channel model that decomposes the end-to-end channel into a smooth regular component and singularity-associated components. The analysis shows that the singularity-associated components become more sensitive to environmental perturbations near singular states. The proposed model also reveals a limitation of conventional water filling (WF): power allocation based only on instan- taneous channel gains does not account for such instability. We therefore estimate singularity information from broadband MIMO channel state information (CSI) and develop a singularity- aware transmit covariance optimization method. Simulations demonstrate numerical stability of the estimated pole frequencies and illustrate the environmental sensitivity of pole-associated channel components. The proposed allocation reduces weighted singular-channel exposure by 90.32% while retaining 95% of the nominal WF rate.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 1. 推导了考虑杂波干扰和通信干扰的ISAC覆盖概率精确解析表达式；2. 推导了低复杂度的基线解析表达式；3. 开发了基于DeepSets的模型用于估计最优UAV-BS位置；4. 首次在无人机网络ISAC性能研究中严格纳入了无人机天线的方向性特征。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个全面的框架来研究无人机网络中的ISAC覆盖概率，通过精确解析表达式刻画了复杂干扰环境下的性能边界，并利用DeepSets深度学习模型实现了智能化的无人机基站位置优化。该研究为6G无人机集群网络中的ISAC系统设计提供了理论基础和技术参考，特别是在考虑实际三维天线方向性方面具有重要创新意义。

Abstract: In unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) networks, integrated communication and sensing (ISAC) is emerging as a key enabler to support extended and continuous communication and sensing capabilities for sixth-generation (6G) services among UAVs operating in swarms. In this paper, a comprehensive framework is presented to investigate the ISAC coverage probability of a reference UAV-base station (BS) in a UAV network. A fixed number of UAV user equipment (UAVUEs) and UAV-BSs are deployed in an aerial corridor modeled as a finite cylinder. The three-dimensional (3D) binomial point process (BPP) is used to model both the 3D spatial locations of the UAV-UEs and the UAV-BSs. By adopting realistic 3D third generation partnership project (3GPP) antenna patterns for both the UAV-UEs and UAV-BSs, the UAVs' boresight direction of the maximum antenna gain is modeled as a random unit vector. All UAV-BSs have sensing capabilities, so an arbitrary yet fixed reference UAV-BS acts as a monostatic radar to acquire sensing measurements associated with a target UAV-UE at a given time snapshot while the target UAVUE communicates with the reference UAV-BS. Consequently, considering both clutter and communication interference, exact analytical expressions for ISAC coverage probability are derived. Low complexity baseline analytical expressions are also derived by ignoring clutter interference and directional characteristics of the UAV-UEs. Finally, motivated by the vision for intelligent UAV networks, a DeepSets-based model is developed and trained to estimate the optimal location of the reference UAV-BS that maximizes ISAC performance given a spatial deployment of the UAVs. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to rigorously investigate ISAC performance in UAV networks while incorporating the directionality characteristics of the UAV antennas.

*Alexander Kelly,Mark F. Flanagan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对RIS辅助空间调制(SM)系统，提出了一种低复杂度的离散相移设计算法。该算法通过缩减的贪婪搜索直接优化接收端信号点间的最小欧氏距离，而非传统的最大化有效信道增益方法。数值结果表明，在相同离散硬件约束下，所提方法相比量化相位对齐方案可获得显著的SNR增益，且该优势在信道状态信息不完美时依然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构智能表面(RIS)与空间调制(SM)结合时，RIS在接收端形成有限个无噪声信号点，最大似然检测下的误码率性能由这些点之间的最小成对欧氏距离决定。然而，现有RIS相移设计方法通常采用相位对齐策略以最大化有效信道增益，这种方法并未直接针对主导错误事件（即最小欧氏距离）进行优化，因此在离散相移约束下的性能受限。此外，连续相移假设在实际硬件中难以实现，需要针对实际离散相移控制设计低复杂度算法。

Method: 本文提出了一种低复杂度的RIS相移设计算法，核心思想包括：(1) 直接针对最小欧氏距离进行优化，而非间接最大化信道增益；(2) 采用缩减的贪婪搜索策略在离散相移集合上进行搜索；(3) 通过一组整数参数控制搜索 effort，实现误码率(BER)性能与计算成本之间的可调权衡；(4) 算法适用于实际离散相移控制约束，区别于理想化的连续相移假设。

Result: 数值评估结果表明：(1) 所提方法在相同离散硬件模型下相比量化相位对齐基线获得了显著的SNR增益；(2) 与最直接可比的基准算法进行复杂度匹配比较时，所提方法仍具优势；(3) 该性能优势在不完美信道状态信息(CSI)条件下依然保持；(4) 通过调整整数参数，可在BER性能与计算复杂度之间实现灵活的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出的低复杂度贪婪搜索算法通过直接优化RIS辅助SM系统中的最小欧氏距离，在离散相移约束下显著优于传统的相位对齐方法。该方法以可控的计算复杂度为代价换取了实质性的性能提升，且对CSI估计误差具有鲁棒性，为实际RIS辅助通信系统的相移设计提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) have emerged as a key enabler for next-generation wireless networks, offering control over the propagation environment through nearly passive reflecting elements. When combined with spatial modulation (SM), an RIS shapes a finite set of noise-free signal points at the receiver, and the error rate performance under maximum-likelihood (ML) detection is governed by the smallest pairwise Euclidean distance among these points. In this paper, we propose a low-complexity RIS phase shift design algorithm for RIS-assisted SM under practical discrete phase shift control. The proposed method targets the minimum Euclidean distance directly through a reduced greedy search over the discrete set of RIS phase shifts, in contrast to existing approaches that align RIS phase shifts to maximize the effective channel gain and therefore do not directly address the dominant error events. The search effort is controlled by a small set of integer parameters, providing a tunable trade-off between bit error rate (BER) performance and computational cost. We evaluate the proposed method against continuous and quantized phase-alignment baselines, including a complexity-matched comparison against the most directly comparable benchmark. Numerical results show that the proposed method achieves a significant SNR gain over quantized phase alignment under the same discrete hardware model, and this advantage persists under imperfect channel state information.

</details>

Conclusion: 该可微分端到端框架实现了超声成像系统参数的联合梯度优化，克服了传统非可微仿真器的优化瓶颈，在仿真和真实数据上均验证了其有效性，为超声成像系统的协同设计提供了高效的计算工具。

Abstract: Ultrasound image quality depends on both the probe geometry and the transmit and receive processing, but engineers usually design the two separately. Physics-based simulators (Field II, k-Wave, MUST/SIMUS) compute the radio-frequency channel data but give no gradients, so design studies must use grid or black-box searches that become slow as the number of parameters increases. We present a fully differentiable end-to-end pipeline for plane-wave ultrasound imaging, with synthetic speckle phantoms, an analytical frequency-domain RF simulator, delay-and-sum beamforming and an extensible image-quality objective. The element pitch, the transmit and receive apodization and the global speed of sound are differentiable parameters of one computation graph, so one reverse-mode pass gives their exact joint gradients. Studies of each parameter rank the design parameters: the optimized pitch decreases the grating lobes, and the speed-of-sound autofocus gives clear contrast and resolution gains. Over 15 test phantoms, the optimized pitch increases the cyst gCNR from 0.55 to 0.66 (about 20%), and the speed-of-sound correction adds a further 0.05. The lateral point-spread function agrees with Field II within about 10%, and on 49 real CUBDL plane-wave acquisitions from five institutions, the framework finds the global speed of sound with a mean absolute difference of 16.6 m/s from the published corrections and phantom speed of sound.

Abstract: Wireless propagation modeling is foundational to the evaluation and design of modern communication systems. To achieve accurate wireless propagation prediction, recent learning-based approaches leverage scene geometry and materials, demonstrating promising generalization across transceiver locations and unseen scenes. However, these approaches remain largely confined to a fixed configuration of array size, receiver orientation, and transmit beam. In this work, we introduce RFWeave, a learned wireless propagation model designed to generalize across scenes, transceiver locations, and transceiver configurations. To accommodate varying array structures, RFWeave incorporates variable-length transceiver tokens that represent antenna elements individually. Furthermore, to capture the geometry between transmit and receive antenna elements, RFWeave aggregates their pairwise spatial relations into propagation tokens. A configuration-conditioned decoder predicts the spatial spectrum and received power. RFWeave is trained on a large-scale simulated dataset spanning 10,000 indoor scenes and more than 5 million channel records under diverse transceiver configurations. Extensive evaluations show that RFWeave achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated generalization settings, improving spatial spectrum structural similarity (SSIM) by up to 15.7% and reducing received power mean absolute error (MAE) by up to 35.4% over the strongest baselines on unseen scenes. Additional experiments on measured DICHASUS data and in beam management further demonstrate RFWeave's broader applicability to real-world channels and downstream wireless tasks.

Abstract: Channel estimation for extremely large-scale uniform planar arrays (UPAs) requires exploiting near-field channel structure to reduce pilot overhead. With a specifically devised channel representation matrix, the near-field channel exhibits a 2D sparse structure, which a conventional 1D block partition might divide into multiple groups. This work develops a 2D block orthogonal matching pursuit (2D-BOMP) estimator that exploits this structure through rectangular coefficient grouping. We establish sufficient recovery conditions for noiseless and noisy measurements and compare the sufficient pilot-length requirements of 1D and 2D partitions. Numerical simulations demonstrate the estimation benefits of 2D-BOMP in estimating the near-field channels of large-scale UPAs, compared with the conventional 1D-BOMP as well as the polar-domain based OMP method.

The primary objective of this paper is to alleviate the double fading constraint in the reflect link without the need for active elements, achieved by leveraging the Fresnel zone of the RIS. The improvement power ratio depends on the frequency and the VSAT 's diameter. The results show that for a VSAT with diameter 1m the gain is up to 9dB at 2 GHz (S band) and up to 14dB at 4 GHz (C band). Additionally, the RIS channel equation is refined, encompassing the tropospheric attenuation and accounting for electromagnetic wave polarization.

Conclusion: 本文提出了PAC框架用于生成式CSI反馈，相比现有基于历史信息的基线方法，PAC在减少约72%-84%反馈开销的同时实现了更高的重建精度，并且在随机游走轨迹下保持CSI重建精度，而现有方法会出现精度急剧下降。

Abstract: Existing deep-learning-based channel state information (CSI) feedback methods reduce feedback overhead by compressing high-dimensional CSI into low-dimensional representations and/or exploiting historical CSI to predict future CSI. However, these methods may become unreliable when user equipment (UE) suddenly changes its motion. To address this issue, we propose a prediction-aided coding (PAC) framework for generative CSI feedback, which adaptively encodes CSI based on predicted channel/CSI distributions and the observed CSI in real time. Each CSI frame is represented by multiple CSI tokens obtained without using historical CSI, making token generation robust to abrupt UE motion. In the first stage, the conditional distribution, instead of a deterministic value, of the next-frame CSI tokens is predicted based on historical tokens. In the second stage, once the next frame arrives, the predicted distribution is used for variable-length entropy coding of its CSI tokens. To further reduce feedback overhead and inference latency, we propose efficient designs for both stages. For the first stage, a customized causal Transformer with a new positional embedding strategy accurately predicts next-frame token probabilities. For the second stage, a parallel causal decoding strategy reduces the inference latency caused by sequential token decoding. Extensive experiments show that, compared with existing history-aware baselines, PAC reduces feedback overhead by approximately 72% to 84% while achieving higher reconstruction accuracy. Additionally, PAC maintains CSI reconstruction accuracy under random-walk trajectories, whereas existing history-aware baselines suffer sharp degradation in reconstruction accuracy.

Abstract: Hybrid analog--digital arrays reduce hardware cost in integrated sensing and communications (ISAC), but spatial compression and beam-dependent scan responses complicate multi-target estimation with few radio-frequency (RF) chains. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a burst beam-scanning multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (MIMO-OFDM) framework that reuses downlink reference signals for joint range, radial-velocity, elevation, and azimuth estimation. Specifically, a structured global--local dual-tensor estimator separates targets by using scanning observations and refines their parameters from selected-beam RF-chain responses, with delay--Doppler signatures maintaining cross-stage target correspondence. The recovered subarray responses yield closed-form elevation and azimuth estimates through exact sum--difference relations, avoiding small-angle approximations and fine angular grid searches. To further improve Doppler accuracy, spatial-mask-aided weighted least squares exploits the longer inter-burst temporal aperture while resolving phase ambiguity using intra-burst estimates. The joint multi-target Cramér--Rao lower bound (CRLB) and corresponding three-dimensional (3D) positioning bound are also derived to characterize estimation performance. Simulation results show that the proposed scheme achieves near-CRLB estimation accuracy at high transmit powers and centimeter- to millimeter-level 3D positioning with only four RF chains, while maintaining low computational cost.

Abstract: Solar-blind ultraviolet (UV) optical wireless communication (OWC) enables non-line-of-sight (NLOS) transmission through atmospheric scattering. This work presents a comprehensive experimental and simulation study of a 100 m outdoor UVC link with comparative measurements under LOS-blocked and open-field conditions. The results show that LOS-blocked scattering-mediated links exhibit substantially higher path loss and narrower bandwidth than corresponding open-field measurements, highlighting the impact of residual LOS components in practical diffused beam UVC experiments. A photon-tracing Monte Carlo (MC) framework incorporating realistic transmitter/receiver characteristics and obstacle geometry is further developed to reproduce the measured channel characteristics with high accuracy. Despite the severe path loss and pulse broadening effects, reliable 20-200 kbps transmission is experimentally demonstrated over the 100 m LOS-blocked link using on-off keying (OOK) and pulse-position modulation (PPM), representing the longest-distance and highest-data-rate experimental demonstration of an explicitly LOS-blocked scattering-mediated UVC link reported to date.

Abstract: This letter studies the throughput and reliability of power-constrained rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) with variable-length incremental-redundancy hybrid automatic repeat request (IR-HARQ) under finite blocklength (FBL) transmission and imperfect channel state information at the transmitter (CSIT). Simulations validate the analysis and quantify the ceiling, offering design insights for delay-robust RSMA under imperfect CSIT.

Abstract: Standard diffusion processes with isotropic noise corruption are not spectrally neutral. They inherit a coarse-to-fine generation hierarchy from the nonuniform data spectrum, because each frequency band loses signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) at its own rate. For signals on a graph, the relevant spectrum is that of the graph shift operator (GSO). A natural construction, therefore, builds both the diffusion dynamics and the denoiser from graph filters of the GSO, and any such design also takes a position on this hierarchy. We expose this hierarchy as a tunable axis through a family of colored score-based diffusions. Fixing the terminal prior to a Gaussian colored by the GSO couples the drift and diffusion through a positive spectral rate, which acts as a clock for each graph-frequency band. The clock sets when a band loses and recovers information, and the forward processes of standard and certain graph-aware diffusions appear as members of this family. A GSO-valued EDM preconditioning splits the denoiser into an analytic Wiener head that carries the Gaussian second-order structure and a graph neural network (GNN) that learns the remaining non-Gaussian dependencies. Numerical experiments show that colored diffusions reach a given generation quality in far fewer sampling steps than standard diffusion.

</details>

Abstract: Many optimization problems that arise in signal processing applications are based around optimizing vectors/matrices of numeric sequences. These problems are often inherently non-convex due to non-linear constraints. Such problems are frequently solved through convex relaxations of the original problem to allow for computationally simple algorithms to be leveraged, albeit with the potential for appreciable performance costs in the solution space. When the constraints of a problem can be implicitly satisfied through the structure of a Riemannian manifold, optimizing directly on said manifold affords the opportunity to solve the problem in an unconstrained fashion. Therefore, when feasible, recasting the constrained optimization problem in Euclidean space as an unconstrained optimization over a Riemannian manifold offers greater flexibility and improved efficiency over convex relaxation based approaches. This paper introduces the minimal smooth optimization theory required and adapts the notation to a radar signal design context. In addition, we apply our approach to two different problems in direct parallel to contemporary convex relaxation-based signal design literature.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a Fisher-information framework for optimizing single-photon detector gating in periodic experiments, in contrast to previous more empirical or heuristic gating choices. After each photon detection, such a detector is insensitive for a dead time, so that an early background detection can obscure a later signal. Gating preserves availability for informative photons by selectively deactivating the detector. In our high-pile-up simulation of distance-attenuated lidar, our practical data-independent gating design reduces the worst-case asymptotic range variance by more than half relative to the free-running acquisition (which does not use any gating). More generally, we characterize achievable detector-active frequencies as a convex polytope and construct an adaptive rule that asymptotically attains an optimal "oracle" design without prior knowledge of the physical parameters. We also derive practical criteria that quantify the potential gains of gating and/or photon attenuation over unattenuated free-running acquisition.

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于时延的波形（OTFS、ODDM、OTSM、OCDM）在多用户上行系统中面临的关键挑战——时间未对齐（TM）问题。作者提出了统一的输入-输出关系框架，分析了TM与保护开销之间的权衡，并与OFDM在多普勒扩展下的性能进行了对比，证明了时延域波形仍具有净性能优势。此外，还评估了多普勒扩展与TM的联合影响以及编码对干扰的缓解作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G无线网络的发展，OTFS、ODDM、OTSM、OCDM等基于时延域的波形被提出以支持超高可靠性和大规模机器类通信等新兴应用。虽然这些波形在高移动性和感知场景中具有显著优势，但在多用户上行系统集成时面临时间未对齐（TM）的关键挑战。与OFDM不同，时延域波形中的残余时间偏移会导致资源的实际移位而非简单的相位旋转，这一问题亟需深入研究。

Method: 本文首先建立了时不变假设下的统一输入-输出关系，将OTFS、ODDM、OTSM、OCDM四种波形统一分析；在此基础上，提出了TM在上行资源分配中的统一分析框架；通过对比分析，将TM在时延域分配中的影响与OFDM中多普勒扩展在频域分配中的影响进行类比；进一步评估了多普勒扩展与TM的联合影响，并通过BER性能分析验证了编码对干扰的缓解效果。

Result: 研究揭示了在TM存在时，干扰抑制与保护开销之间存在根本性权衡；时延域波形在考虑保护开销后仍能保持相对于OFDM的净性能优势；TM在时延域分配中的行为与多普勒扩展在频域分配中的行为具有类比关系；编码可以有效缓解TM引起的干扰；该研究为未来时延域波形的补偿和调度策略开发建立了问题框架。

Conclusion: 本文建立了基于时延域波形的多用户上行系统中TM问题的统一分析框架，证明了时延域波形在TM条件下仍优于OFDM，并揭示了干扰抑制与保护开销的权衡关系。研究成果为未来6G系统中时延域波形的实用化多用户操作提供了理论基础，激励了补偿机制和调度策略的进一步研究。

Abstract: The shift towards 6G wireless networks calls for advanced waveforms to support emerging applications such as ultra-reliable and massive machine-type communications. Among the proposed candidates, OTFS, ODDM, OTSM, and OCDM share the common feature of exploiting the delay domain for data transmission. While these waveforms offer significant advantages in high-mobility and sensing scenarios, their integration into multi-user uplink systems reveals a critical challenge: timing misalignment (TM). Unlike OFDM, where residual timing offsets translate into phase rotations, delay-based waveforms experience actual shifts of resources, whose impact we investigate in this work. We show that the aforementioned waveforms can be unified under a time-invariant setup through a common input--output relation, which enables a unified analysis of TM in uplink resource allocation. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between interference mitigation and guard overhead in the presence of TM. We further benchmark the observed behavior against OFDM under Doppler spread as a reference impairment and highlight the analogy between TM in delay-based allocation and Doppler spread in frequency-based allocation. The results show that delay-based waveforms can maintain a net performance advantage over OFDM in the considered setup. Beyond this unified Doppler-free analysis, we evaluate the joint impact of Doppler spread and TM on each waveform individually. We also investigate BER performance and show how coding can mitigate the resulting interference. This study therefore establishes a problem framework that motivates the development of future compensation and scheduling strategies for practical multi-user operation of delay-based waveforms.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文比较了标定传播几何与成对训练数据作为先验信息用于XL-RIS虚拟观测重建的效果，提出的OG-NF-SPR方法在高信噪比匹配条件下优于深度学习网络，但在强传播失配且训练覆盖充分时数据驱动方法更优，同时时间正则化显著提升了位置估计精度。
