  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极大规模可重构智能表面(XL-RIS)为近场感知提供了位置敏感的孔径，但其逐单元信号无法直接观测。从较少的基站测量中恢复这种虚拟RIS观测(VOR)需要利用先验信息。本文旨在探究标定传播几何信息与成对训练数据这两种先验信息，哪种对于重建更为有效。

Method: 本文针对共同的感知算子和目标信号，提出了离网近场稀疏参数重建方法(OG-NF-SPR)，并与基于CompactNet和DenseNet的深度重建网络进行对比。OG-NF-SPR采用反射路径投影进行位置初始化，随后通过贝叶斯估计所有路径增益并进行连续位置精化。此外，还提出了一种因果扩展方法，能够在无需轨迹标签的情况下利用时间相关性。受控仿真实验以近场无人机跟踪为例，分别考察了噪声、位置分布偏移、标定误差和传播失配等因素的影响。

Result: 在20dB匹配传播条件下，OG-NF-SPR的重建归一化均方误差(NMSE)比两种基准深度网络低约67%。在低信噪比条件下，统计平均比选择单一几何解释更有效。在足够强的可重复失配条件下，针对失配族训练的同等规模网络优于标称物理估计器，而标称训练则无法提供此优势。时间正则化还将共享轨迹上的位置均方根误差(RMSE)从0.653m降低至0.225m。

Conclusion: 研究结果将重建性能与测量质量、标定精度和训练覆盖范围相关联，为几何信息何时可以替代监督标签以及额外数据何时有用提供了指导。总体而言，在传播模型准确且信噪比较高时，基于几何的稀疏参数重建方法优于深度学习方法；而在存在系统性传播失配且训练数据充分覆盖该失配族时，数据驱动方法更具优势。

Abstract: A passive extremely large-scale reconfigurable intelligent surface (XL-RIS) offers a position-sensitive aperture for near-field sensing, but its element-wise signals are not directly observed. Recovering this virtual RIS observation (VOR) from fewer base-station measurements requires prior information. This paper investigates when calibrated propagation geometry or paired training data provides the more effective reconstruction prior. For a common sensing operator and target signal, we develop off-grid near-field sparse parametric reconstruction (OG-NF-SPR) and compare it with compact and DenseNet-based deep reconstructors. OG-NF-SPR uses reflected-path projection for position initialization, followed by Bayesian estimation of all path gains and continuous position refinement. A causal extension incorporates temporal correlation without trajectory labels. Controlled simulations use near-field UAV tracking as an illustration and separately examine noise, position-distribution shift, calibration error, and propagation mismatch. Under matched propagation at 20 dB, OG-NF-SPR achieves approximately 67% lower reconstruction NMSE than the two nominal deep networks. At low SNR, statistical averaging is more effective than selecting a single geometric explanation. Under sufficiently strong repeatable mismatch, a same-size network trained on the mismatch family outperforms the nominal physical estimator, whereas nominal training does not provide this benefit. Temporal regularization also reduces position RMSE from 0.653 m to 0.225 m on a shared trajectory. The results relate reconstruction performance to measurement quality, calibration accuracy, and training coverage, providing guidance on when geometry can replace supervised labels and when additional data are useful.

</details>

*Salim Chabani,Riadh Ouzane,Salah Eddine Zegrar,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Shuf-Scatter是一种针对OFDM环境反向散射通信的新型干扰抑制方案，通过对反射信号进行确定性置换创建相关域签名，利用准正交性消除直达链路干扰，无需额外频谱或信道状态信息即可实现强干扰下的可靠检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于正交频分复用(OFDM)的环境反向散射通信(AmBC)系统中，直达链路干扰(DLI)是一个主要瓶颈，严重制约了系统性能。现有方法通常需要专用频谱资源或瞬时反向散射链路(BL)信道状态信息(CSI)，增加了系统复杂度和开销。

Method: 本文提出Shuf-Scatter方案，通过对反射的OFDM波形应用确定性置换(permutation)，在相关域创建一个独特的签名。接收机利用重排后的反向散射信号与直达链路分量之间的准正交性，有效抑制DLI干扰。

Result: 分析和仿真结果表明，Shuf-Scatter方案在强DLI条件下实现了优异的误码率(BER)性能，同时保持了传统OFDM传输的完整性。该方案无需专用频谱资源，也不需要瞬时反向散射链路信道状态信息。

Conclusion: Shuf-Scatter通过确定性置换和相关域处理，为OFDM-based AmBC系统提供了一种高效、低复杂度的DLI抑制方案，在强干扰环境下实现了可靠的反向散射信号检测，同时兼容传统OFDM传输。

Abstract: Direct Link Interference (DLI) remains a major bottleneck in Orthogonal Frequency Division Multiplexing (OFDM)-based Ambient Backscatter Communication (AmBC) systems. This letter proposes Shuf-Scatter, a scheme that applies a deterministic permutation to the reflected OFDM waveform to create a distinct correlation-domain signature. By exploiting the quasi-orthogonality between the reshuffled backscatter signal and the direct-link component, the proposed receiver effectively mitigates DLI and enables reliable detection without requiring dedicated spectral resources, or instantaneous Backscatter-Link (BL) Channel State Information (CSI). Analytical and simulation results demonstrate that Shuf-Scatter achieves superior Bit Error Rate (BER) performance under strong DLI while preserving the integrity of the legacy OFDM transmission.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出基于跨图像路由注意力(CIRA)的CiFormer网络，利用双端点RGB图像进行mmWave波束对功率预测，显著提升了波束选择准确率，验证了链路中心感知在感知辅助通信中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单端点感知仅能提供传播环境的部分视图，需要链路中心感知以获取双端点的完整传播信息，从而提升感知辅助通信的性能。

Method: 提出跨图像路由注意力机制(CIRA)，用于从双端点RGB图像中进行稀疏关联和相互增强传播相关特征；基于CIRA构建CiFormer网络，预测mmWave波束对功率矩阵。

Result: CiFormer相比最强基线降低了平均绝对误差，并将top-1波束对选择准确率提升了3.15个百分点；可视化结果和消融实验验证了双端点感知和选择性跨视角交互在捕获传播相关依赖性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CiFormer通过双端点感知和选择性跨视角交互，在mmWave波束预测任务中取得了优于最强基线的性能，且该方法在无线通信任务中具有更广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: For sensing-aided communications, single-endpoint sensing offers only a partial view of propagation, motivating link-centric sensing across both endpoints. We propose cross-image routing attention (CIRA) for sparse association and mutual enhancement of propagation-relevant features from dual-endpoint RGB images. Building on CIRA, CiFormer predicts millimeter-wave (mmWave) beam-pair power matrices for vehicle-to-infrastructure (V2I) communications. Experiments show that CiFormer reduces mean absolute error and improves top-1 beam-pair selection accuracy by 3.15 percentage points over the strongest baseline. Visualizations and ablation studies support dual-endpoint sensing and selective cross-view interaction in capturing propagation-relevant dependencies, suggesting broader applications in wireless tasks beyond beam prediction.

</details>


### [52] [Overcoming Catastrophic Forgetting: Continual Learning for Radar Waveform Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18404)
*Mohamed Rabie,Chinthana J. Panagamuwa,Konstantinos G. Kyriakopoulos*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 首个大规模雷达波形识别持续学习基准，评估18种方法发现数据回放最有效，机制组合可提升F1分数2.7-5.5%，为雷达波形分类系统设计提供机制级依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动雷达波形识别需要对新出现的波形变体进行持续学习，而无需从头重新训练。然而，学习新波形时会发生灾难性遗忘，导致先前知识被覆盖。虽然持续学习方法可以应对这一挑战，但现有雷达研究仅在狭窄基准上评估了少数方法，实践者缺乏关于应部署哪种方法或哪些机制有效的证据。

Method: 本研究构建了首个大规模雷达波形识别持续学习基准。在任务增量（TIL）和类增量（CIL）设置以及单轮和多轮训练机制下，对18种持续学习方法在包含54种波形的四个公共数据集的顺序十任务问题上进行评估。对领先方法的内部机制进行消融实验和统计检验，每个消融与其完整方法在n=6个训练种子上进行对比，结果不确定时增加至n=12，并在每个方法族内进行Holm-Bonferroni校正。

Result: 保留旧任务数据的方法在TIL和CIL设置中均领先。简单回放（plain replay）在TIL下可缩小下限与实际上限之间差距的77-88%，但在CIL下仅缩小46-55%。消融结果显示多个机制带来较小但统计显著的增益。结合这些机制后，TIL和CIL设置下的领先F1分数分别提升3.4-5.5%和2.7-4.9%，且组合模型在所有信噪比的两种设置下均超越所有基准方法。

Conclusion: 本研究为雷达波形分类系统研究者提供了机制层面的设计依据，首次大规模系统评估了持续学习方法在雷达波形识别中的有效性，并通过严格的统计检验识别了有效机制，证明了机制组合可以显著提升性能。

Abstract: Automated radar waveform recognition classifies the waveform and modulation type of a received signal. As new waveform variants appear over time, a deployed classifier must learn them without retraining from scratch. Catastrophic Forgetting occurs when learning new waveforms overwrites prior knowledge. Continual Learning (CL) methods tackle this challenge, but prior radar studies only evaluate a few methods on narrow benchmarks. Practitioners lack evidence on which method to deploy or which mechanisms are effective. This work presents the first large-scale benchmark of continual learning for radar waveform recognition. Eighteen CL methods are evaluated on a sequential ten-task problem, drawn from four public datasets spanning 54 waveforms, under task- and class-incremental (TIL, CIL) settings and single- and multi-epoch regimes. Methods that retain data from old tasks lead both TIL and CIL settings. Plain replay alone closes 77-88% of the gap between the lower and upper practical limits under TIL, but only 46-55% under CIL. The internal mechanisms of the leading methods are ablated and statistically tested to identify effective mechanisms. Each ablation is paired against its full method over n=6 training seeds, topped up to n=12 when inconclusive, and p-values are Holm-Bonferroni corrected within each method's family. Ablation results show that several mechanisms confer smaller but statistically significant gains. Combining these mechanisms improves the leading F1 score by 3.4-5.5% and 2.7-4.9% under TIL and CIL, respectively, and the combined models exceed every benchmarked method in both settings for all SNRs. These results give researchers of radar waveform classification systems a mechanism-level basis for design.

</details>

*Bangyan Xia,Bo Tan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三轴里德伯原子接收器，利用三个正交射频本地振荡器产生可分辨的拍频系数，通过校准映射构建拍频矢量观测，结合最大似然估计实现高灵敏度的射频信号到达方向估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频信号源到达方向(DoA)估计需要高灵敏度的接收方案。传统方法在弱射频场检测和方向估计方面存在局限性，需要探索基于新型物理机制的高灵敏度DoA传感方案。

Method: 本文开发了一种三轴里德伯原子接收器模型。该方法使用三个名义正交的射频本地振荡器(LO)，每个分配不同的频率偏移，将弱入射射频场投影到可分辨的光学读出复数拍频系数中。通过响应校准机制将系数映射到笛卡尔坐标系的三轴上，获得拍频矢量观测。对于已知的圆极化射频信号，该观测产生方向相关的里德伯导向矢量，从而实现最大似然DoA估计。

Result: 仿真结果表明，痕量级拍频提取支持所提出的DoA感知工作流程，并为与传统矢量天线(VA)基准的比较提供了基础。所提出的方法将里德伯拍频矢量前端转换为高灵敏度射频场DoA传感器。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种基于三轴里德伯原子接收器的DoA估计方法，通过拍频矢量观测和最大似然估计实现了高灵敏度的射频场方向传感，为里德伯原子在射频传感领域的应用提供了新的技术途径，并展示了相对于传统矢量天线的潜在优势。

Abstract: This paper develops a three-axis Rydberg atomic receiver model for direction-of-arrival (DoA) estimation of the radio frequency (RF) signal source. Three nominally orthogonal RF local oscillators (LOs), each assigned a distinct frequency offset, project the weak incident RF field into resolvable complex beat coefficients in the optical readout. A beat-vector observation is obtained by adopting a response calibration mechanism that maps the coefficients onto the three axes of the Cartesian coordinate system. For a known circularly polarised RF signal, this observation induces a direction-dependent Rydberg steering vector, enabling maximum-likelihood DoA estimation. The pro posed procedure translates the Rydberg beat-vector front end into a highly sensitive RF field DoA sensor. Simulation results show that trace-level beat extraction supports the proposed DoA sensing workflow and provides a basis for comparison with a traditional vector antenna (VA) benchmark.

</details>


### [54] [Task-evoked eye movements as candidate digital biomarkers of depression severity in adolescents and young adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18494)
*Youxiang Sun,Yujie Nie,Zhuoru Wu,Mengyuan Yu,Wenkong Wang,Xiang Li,Yumei Wang,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究通过情绪-空间任务的眼动追踪发现，抑郁严重程度与视觉探索模式改变（如注视熵降低、目标导向性减弱）相关，而非负性注意偏向；机器学习分类器可实现中度准确的严重程度分层，为抑郁症数字化评估提供新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统抑郁症严重程度评估依赖主观症状报告，存在时效性不足的问题。研究旨在探索任务诱发眼动是否能提供可解释的行为标记，以补充传统评估方法并促进更及时的临床评估。

Method: 单中心横断面研究，纳入155名14-20岁青少年和年轻成人。参与者完成情绪-空间自由观看任务和记忆引导回忆任务，按17项汉密尔顿抑郁评定量表评分分层。使用协变量调整的序数趋势模型，在六个任务知情域中检验19个预设眼动特征。同时采用机器学习方法，结合44个候选眼动特征和人口学协变量构建三级级联分类器。

Result: 抑郁严重程度与以下眼动特征显著相关（FDR校正后q<0.001）：注视分配熵降低（标准化β=-0.599）、区域间切换次数减少（β=-0.526）、首次目标进入潜伏期延长（β=0.572）、目标停留比例降低（β=-0.678）。情绪特异性注意偏向与严重程度无关；注视分散度与严重程度的关联在调整焦虑症状后减弱。三级级联分类器实现最佳四类分层性能：准确率0.665、宏F1分数0.670、ROC曲线下面积0.869，优于单级和两级方法。

Conclusion: 研究识别出视觉探索和记忆引导注视控制的改变是抑郁症严重程度的候选数字生物标记，而非广泛的负性注意偏向。这些发现支持进一步前瞻性评估任务诱发眼动追踪作为严重程度评估和临床决策支持的补充方法。

Abstract: Objective digital measures of depression severity could complement conventional symptom assessments and facilitate more timely clinical evaluation. Here, we investigated whether task-evoked eye movements provide interpretable behavioral markers of depression severity in adolescents and young adults. In this single-center, cross-sectional study, 155 participants aged 14--20 years completed emotional-spatial free-viewing and memory-guided recall tasks and were stratified by 17-item Hamilton Depression Rating Scale scores. We examined 19 prespecified eye-movement features across six task-informed domains using covariate-adjusted ordinal-trend models. Greater depression severity was associated with lower gaze-allocation entropy (standardized $β=-0.599$), fewer inter-area switches ($β=-0.526$), longer first target-entry latency ($β=0.572$), and lower target dwell proportion ($β=-0.678$; all false-discovery-rate-adjusted $q<0.001$). Emotion-specific attentional-bias measures were not associated with severity, whereas associations involving fixation dispersion weakened after adjustment for anxiety symptoms. In a complementary machine-learning analysis incorporating 44 candidate eye-movement features and demographic covariates, a three-stage cascade classifier achieved the highest four-class stratification performance (accuracy, 0.665; macro-F1, 0.670; area under the receiver operating characteristic curve, 0.869), outperforming single-stage and two-stage approaches. Together, these findings identify altered visual exploration and memory-guided gaze control as candidate digital biomarkers of depression severity, rather than generalized negative attentional bias. These results support further prospective evaluation of task-evoked eye tracking as a complementary approach for timely severity assessment and clinical decision support.

</details>


### [55] [Contactless gait-derived features as candidate digital biomarkers of depression severity in adolescents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18510)
*Shuai Liu,Xiang Li,Guangxin Dai,Mengyuan Yu,Wenkong Wang,Hanchi Liu,Jiahui Niu,Yujie Nie,Yumei Wang,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究构建了青少年抑郁症步态数据集，开发了基于图卷积网络的步态分析模型UMLPA-GCN，实现了75.63%的抑郁症严重程度分类准确率，并识别出手臂摆动减少、骨盆运动减慢等潜在数字生物标志物，为青少年抑郁症的无接触式客观评估提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青少年抑郁症严重程度的及时评估对指导临床护理至关重要，但传统评估依赖于间歇性临床评估和症状评定量表，存在局限性。因此，需要一种可扩展的客观行为特征捕捉方法来实现抑郁症的无接触式评估。

Method: 研究构建了长距离三维青少年抑郁症步态数据集（LD3D-ADG），包含119名青少年的深度传感器步态记录（39名健康对照、41名轻度抑郁、39名中重度抑郁）。开发了UMLPA-GCN图卷积网络模型，该模型基于三个解剖学定义的骨骼分区对时空步态动态进行建模。采用参与者独立的五折交叉验证进行评估。

Result: UMLPA-GCN模型在分类任务中达到75.63%的准确率，宏F1分数为75.52%，宏ROC曲线下面积为0.839（95%置信区间：0.779-0.899）。各类别召回率分别为：健康对照87.18%、轻度抑郁63.41%、中重度抑郁76.92%。在参与者层面，识别出减少的手臂摆动、较慢的骨盆运动、受限的关节活动范围以及更大的手臂摆动不对称性作为潜在的数字生物标志物，这些特征与较高的17项汉密尔顿抑郁评定量表评分相关。

Conclusion: 该研究为青少年抑郁症严重程度的无接触式数字分层提供了概念验证证据。然而，仍需进一步的外部验证和前瞻性验证，以确定其在纵向监测和及时临床评估中的实用性。

Abstract: Timely assessment of depression severity in adolescents is essential for guiding clinical care, yet conventional evaluations rely on episodic clinical assessments and symptom rating scales. Contactless gait analysis offers a potentially scalable approach to capturing objective behavioral features associated with depression. Here, we introduce the Long-Distance 3D Adolescent Depression Gait Dataset (LD3D-ADG), comprising depth-sensor gait recordings from 119 adolescents at a single center, including 39 healthy controls, 41 with mild depression, and 39 with moderate-to-severe depression. We developed UMLPA-GCN, a graph convolutional network that models spatiotemporal gait dynamics across three anatomically defined skeletal partitions. In participant-independent five-fold cross-validation, UMLPA-GCN achieved 75.63% classification accuracy, a macro-F1 score of 75.52%, and a macro-area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.839 (95% confidence interval, 0.779--0.899). Class-specific recall was 87.18%, 63.41%, and 76.92% for healthy controls, mild depression, and moderate-to-severe depression, respectively. At the participant level, reduced arm swing, slower pelvic movement, restricted joint range of motion, and greater arm-swing asymmetry were identified as leading potential digital biomarkers contributing to depression severity assessment, associated with higher 17-item Hamilton Depression Rating Scale scores. These findings provide proof-of-concept evidence for contactless digital depression severity stratification in adolescents. Further external and prospective validation is needed to establish their utility for longitudinal monitoring and timely clinical assessment.

</details>


### [56] [RetinaSC: Retinal Semantic Communication Framework for Diabetic Retinopathy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18824)
*Avani Shinod,Rangineni Rushitha,Asqeem Nas,Anirban Bhowal,Shikha Baghel*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）分级的轻量级语义通信框架，基于APTOS2019眼底图像数据集，采用多种深度学习架构（ResNet34、U-Net、MobileNetV2、EfficientNet-B0）构建语义编码器，并通过迁移学习扩展至IDRiD数据集，旨在实现DR的早期检测以辅助远程医疗。实验表明，MobileNetV2在复杂度、延迟、吞吐量和F1分数之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）是导致失明的主要原因之一，早期检测对于预防完全失明至关重要。传统通信方式在传输医学图像时面临带宽限制和延迟问题，而远程医疗领域亟需高效、低延迟的解决方案来实现DR的及时诊断。因此，需要开发一种轻量级的语义通信框架，能够在保证诊断性能的同时降低传输开销，特别针对APTOS2019等眼底图像数据集进行优化。

Method: 1. 构建轻量级语义通信框架，包含语义编码器模块；2. 采用四种深度学习架构设计语义编码器：ResNet34、U-Net、MobileNetV2和EfficientNet-B0；3. 基于APTOS2019数据集进行模型训练；4. 使用迁移学习方法将训练好的模型应用于IDRiD数据集；5. 评估任务导向和重建导向两种语义通信模式；6. 综合考虑模型复杂度、端到端延迟、吞吐量和分类性能（加权F1分数）进行模型选型分析。

Result: 1. 在APTOS2019数据集上，基于ResNet34和EfficientNet-B0的任务导向语义系统在不同信道条件下均实现了超过0.8的加权F1分数，表现出令人满意的鲁棒性；2. MobileNetV2在复杂度、延迟、吞吐量和F1分数之间取得了最佳权衡：任务导向和重建导向模块的加权F1分数均为0.76-0.78，端到端延迟仅为8.162毫秒；3. 验证了迁移学习在IDRiD数据集上的有效性，证明了框架的可扩展性；4. 结果表明轻量级语义通信框架适用于远程医疗场景中的实时DR筛查。

Conclusion: 本文提出的轻量级语义通信框架能够有效支持糖尿病视网膜病变的自动分级，其中MobileNetV2因其低延迟（8.162ms）和可接受的分类性能（加权F1约0.77）成为最适合实际部署的模型。该框架通过语义通信减少了冗余数据传输，结合迁移学习能力可扩展至不同数据集，为远程医疗中的DR早期检测提供了可行的技术方案，有助于预防患者完全失明。未来可进一步优化语义编码策略以在更低带宽下保持诊断精度。

Abstract: This paper presents a lightweight semantic com- munication framework for diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading from fundus images pertaining specifically to the APTOS2019 dataset. The framework includes semantic encoder designed using ResNet34, U-Net, MobileNetV2, and EfficientNet-B0 architectures. Further, the trained models are used to train the same framework for IDRiD dataset using transfer learning approach. Such a framework will enable the telemedicine sector in the early detection of DR and prevent total blindness. The results reveal that for APTOS, task-oriented semantic systems can achieve satisfactory robust performance (more than 0.8 weighted F1 score for ResNet34 and EfficientNet-B0) across different channels. In particular, considering the trade-off between complexity, latency, throughput, and F1 score, MobileNetV2 is the best model with weighted F1 score of 0.76-0.78 for both task and reconstruction- oriented modules and end-to-end latency of only 8.162 ms.

</details>

### [57] [Semantic Edge-Cloud Communication for Real-Time Urban Traffic Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14187)
*Murat Arda Onsu,Arman Dogru,Poonam Lohan,Burak Kantarci,Aisha Syed*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语义通信的边缘-云端框架，通过传输任务相关语义信息替代原始视频，实现城市交通数字孪生的实时同步，节省99%以上传输量且轨迹重建误差仅0.12米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通系统日益复杂，需要实时交通感知、主动碰撞预测和智能管理以提升安全性并缓解拥堵。AI驱动的交通管理虽有前景，但依赖涵盖碰撞、近碰撞及违规等关键场景的高质量多样化数据。数字孪生技术能同步构建物理交通环境的虚拟副本，实现实时监控、碰撞预测、交通流优化及多样化真实场景生成。然而，通过边缘监控摄像头持续同步高保真城市交通数字孪生需要大量边缘-云端通信，传输原始视频流会导致严重的带宽和延迟瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种基于语义通信的边缘-云端框架，用于实时城市交通数字孪生应用。该框架利用边缘智能提取并仅传输与任务相关的语义交通信息，而非完整视频流，从而在保持准确实时数字孪生重建所需关键动态信息的同时，显著降低通信开销。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架实现超过99%的传输节省，平均轨迹重建误差仅为0.12米。

Conclusion: 该框架被证实是一种有效、可扩展且低延迟的解决方案，适用于AI赋能的城市交通数字孪生应用。

Abstract: Urban traffic systems are becoming increasingly complex, demanding real-time traffic perception, proactive collision prediction, and intelligent management to improve safety and reduce congestion. Although AI-driven traffic management has shown strong promise, its success relies on diverse, high-quality data spanning critical scenarios such as collisions, near-collisions, and traffic rule violations. Digital twins offer a powerful solution by enabling synchronized virtual replicas of physical traffic environments for real-time monitoring, collision prediction, traffic-flow optimization, and diverse realistic scenario generation. However, continuously synchronizing a high-fidelity urban traffic digital twin through edge surveillance cameras requires substantial edge-cloud communication, and transmitting raw video streams leads to severe bandwidth and latency constraints. To overcome this challenge, this paper proposes a semantic communication-based edge-cloud framework for real-time urban traffic digital twin applications. By leveraging edge intelligence to extract and transmit only task-relevant semantic traffic information instead of full video feeds, the proposed framework significantly reduces communication overhead while preserving the essential dynamics needed for accurate real-time digital twin reconstruction. Experimental results demonstrate more than 99% transmission savings and an average trajectory reconstruction error of only 0.12 m, confirming the proposed framework as an effective, scalable, and low-latency solution for AI-enabled urban traffic digital twin applications.

</details>


### [58] [An Availability Management Framework for Microservice-Based Safety-Critical CIoT Systems: Architecture and End-to-End Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14230)
*Hassaan Siddiqui,Ferhat Khendek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可用性管理框架(AMF)，通过结合机器学习异常检测和主动式热备切换，解决云原生编排平台中被动故障恢复导致的多秒级服务中断问题，并在远程驾驶测试平台上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键型蜂窝物联网(CIoT)系统日益依赖跨异构边缘和云平台编排的微服务架构。虽然该范式提升了模块化和可扩展性，但现有编排平台的容错机制仍以被动式为主，即仅在故障发生后才重启或重新调度失败组件。这种恢复方式会产生多秒的端到端服务中断时间(E2E SOT)，且在容器重启退避机制下可能进一步恶化，这对于安全关键型应用是不可接受的。

Method: 本文提出了可用性管理框架(AMF)，一个通用的监控-预测-恢复闭环框架。该方法的核心创新包括：(1) 采用基于机器学习的异常检测实现主动式故障预测；(2) 采用make-before-break(先建后断)机制实现向备用副本的主动切换；(3) 在三种协议(包括运行工况变化)下离线评估候选ML模型以选择适合实时部署的模型；(4) 将AMF实例化部署于三层远程驾驶(ToD)测试平台，并在阶段性资源耗尽场景下进行验证。

Result: 在三层远程驾驶测试平台上，相对于仅使用Kubernetes的被动恢复机制，AMF能够在异常窗口期内完成切换，显著降低E2E SOT，并避免了退避机制下重复原地重启导致的数十秒级中断。在该非实时测试平台上，残余E2E SOT处于健康路径抖动范围内，因此受限于路径时延而非恢复决策的滞后性。研究表明，将经过验证的基于ML的异常检测器与主动式切换相结合，可以从关键路径中消除由重启和退避机制主导的服务中断。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过耦合经验证的机器学习异常检测器与主动式热备切换，可以有效消除安全关键型云原生系统中由被动重启和退避机制导致的服务中断。AMF框架展示了在异常窗口内完成服务切换的可行性，使得残余中断时间仅受限于网络路径时延而非恢复决策延迟。该工作为构建高可用、低中断的安全关键型蜂窝物联网系统提供了有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Safety-critical Cellular Internet of Things (CIoT) systems increasingly rely on microservice architectures orchestrated across heterogeneous edge and cloud platforms. Although this paradigm improves modularity and scalability, the fault-tolerance mechanisms of contemporary orchestration platforms remain predominantly reactive, i.e., failed components are restarted or rescheduled only after an outage has already occurred. Such recovery can produce multi-second End to End Service Outage Time (E2E SOT), which can grow further under container restart backoff. This paper proposes an Availability Management Framework (AMF), a generic monitor-predict-recover loop that complements reactive recovery with machine learning-based anomaly detection and make-before-break switchover to a standby replica. Candidate ML models are evaluated offline under three protocols, including a change of operating regime, in order to select a model for live deployment. AMF is instantiated and evaluated on a three-tier tele-operated driving (ToD) testbed under a staged resource-exhaustion scenario. Relative to Kubernetes-only recovery, AMF completes switchover during the anomaly window, reduces E2E SOT, and avoids the tens-of-seconds outages associated with repeated in-place restarts under backoff. On this non-real-time testbed, residual E2E SOT lies inside healthy-path jitter and is therefore limited by path timing rather than by lateness of the recovery decision. The paper whows that coupling a validated ML based anomaly detector with proactive switchover can remove restart- and backoff-dominated outage from a critical path.

</details>


### [59] [Harvesting-Aware Routing for Low-Latency Reliable Transmission in Interference-Powered Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14250)
*Azadeh Sheikholeslami,Allan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对纯射频能量收集的多跳无线网络，提出HOPE和THOPE两种最优算法，将联合路由与功率分配问题转化为Dijkstra可解的最小代价路径问题，实现端到端时延最小化且对干扰密度具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多跳无线网络中，能量收集节点完全依赖环境射频干扰作为唯一能量来源。研究目标是在瑞利衰落信道下，以最小化端到端时延为目标，同时保证目标端到端中断概率，从而将干扰的双重角色（既提供可收集能量，又损害链路可靠性）纳入联合优化框架。

Method: 首先将复杂的乘性端到端中断约束转化为加性形式。然后提出两种最优算法：(1) HOPE算法——通过将问题规约为具有干扰感知链路权重的最小代价路径计算，最小化瓶颈收集时延（路径上最长能量累积时间）；(2) THOPE算法——将目标扩展为总端到端时延（增加累积传输时延），同样可得到闭式最小代价路径解。两种算法均通过Dijkstra算法在多项式时间内求解，并导出最优发射功率的显式表达式。

Result: HOPE和THOPE算法相比最短路径和最小能量基准方案，平均总时延降低可达一个数量级；同时其时延分布高度集中，且对干扰源密度变化具有显著的鲁棒性（不敏感性）。

Conclusion: 通过将乘性中断约束转化为加性形式，可将联合路由与功率分配问题高效地规约为经典的最短路径问题。所提HOPE和THOPE算法在多项式时间内获得最优解，在显著降低时延的同时保证了鲁棒的性能表现，为能量收集无线网络中的干扰管理与资源优化提供了有效的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: We consider a multi-hop wireless network where energy-harvesting nodes rely solely on ambient radio-frequency interference as their power source. The goal is to deliver a packet from source to destination with minimum latency while guaranteeing a target end-to-end outage probability under Rayleigh fading. This gives rise to a joint routing and power allocation problem in which interference plays a dual role: it provides harvestable energy, but also impairs link reliability. We first transform the intricate, multiplicative end-to-end outage constraint into an additive form. Then, we propose two optimal algorithms. The first, HOPE, minimizes the bottleneck harvesting latency, i.e., the longest energy-accumulation time along the path, by reducing the problem to a least-cost path computation on a graph with interference-aware link weights. The second, THOPE, extends the objective to total end-to-end latency, adding the cumulative transmission delay, and likewise admits a closed-form least-cost path solution. Both algorithms are solved in polynomial time via Dijkstra's algorithm and yield explicit expressions for the optimal transmit powers. Extensive simulations reveal that HOPE and THOPE reduce the average total latency by up to an order of magnitude compared with shortest-path and minimum-energy benchmarks, while exhibiting sharply concentrated delay distributions that are largely insensitive to interferer density.

</details>


### [60] [RADIUS-Assisted Companion PSK Discovery: Enabling WPA3-SAE in Externally Authenticated Multi-PSK (ePSK / MPSK / DPSK / iPSK / PPSK) Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14410)
*Azif Abdul Salam Kolisam Beevi,Anil Kumar Reddy,Niraj Lochan Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出RADIUS辅助的Companion PSK发现方案(RA-CAPD)，解决了先前CAPD方案中AP必须本地存储所有PSK的假设限制，使得WPA3-SAE能够在多PSK网络中通过外部RADIUS服务器实现安全的PSK发现与客户端迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多住户单元(MDU)、学生宿舍和酒店网络通常将每住户的PSK存储在外部RADIUS服务器或云数据库中，而非AP本地。现有CAPD方案假设AP能够自行测试所有PSK，这一假设在实际部署中存在局限性，因为PSK可能由外部系统管理且数量庞大。

Method: 提出RA-CAPD方案：AP将ANonce、EAPOL-Key消息2(SNonce、MIC、RSNE)、SSID、客户端MAC、BSSID和AP MAC通过RADIUS厂商特定属性(VSA)转发至外部服务器；服务器对所有预配置密钥验证MIC，识别匹配的PSK，并使用RADIUS共享密钥(或云数据库的每AP密钥)加密返回PSK，同时返回单元/房间/用户标识和VLAN信息；AP将客户端绑定到该PSK和身份，并在域内所有AP间同步绑定，然后通过802.11v BSS切换管理请求(支持11v的客户端)或断开重连(其他客户端)将客户端迁移至配置配置文件。

Result: 在配置文件上，支持SAE的客户端完成WPA3-SAE，仅支持WPA2的客户端完成WPA2-PSK，均使用已学习的密钥且无需进一步搜索，Companion BSSID仅用于接入引导。方案已在商用AP上完成实验室验证。

Conclusion: RA-CAPD成功移除了AP本地测试所有PSK的假设，通过RADIUS协议安全地利用外部服务器进行PSK发现，实现了多PSK网络中WPA3-SAE的部署，同时保持了安全性和可操作性，适用于大规模MDU、学生宿舍和酒店网络场景。

Abstract: Large multi-dwelling unit (MDU), student-housing and hospitality networks rarely store resident pre-shared keys (PSKs) on the access point (AP). Instead, the per-resident keys of ePSK, MPSK, DPSK, iPSK or PPSK services are held in an external RADIUS server or cloud database synchronised with the property-management system. Our earlier work, Companion-BSSID-Assisted PSK Discovery (CAPD), brought WPA3-SAE to multi-PSK networks by learning the client's PSK on a WPA2-only companion BSSID and steering the client with IEEE 802.11v to a hidden WPA2/WPA3 primary BSSID of the same SSID. CAPD assumes that the AP can test every PSK itself. This paper removes that assumption. We propose RADIUS-Assisted Companion PSK Discovery (RA-CAPD). The AP forwards the ANonce, the raw EAPOL-Key message 2 (SNonce, MIC, RSNE), the SSID, the client MAC, the BSSID and the AP MAC to the external server in RADIUS vendor-specific attributes (VSAs). The server validates the MIC against all provisioned keys, identifies the matching PSK, and returns it encrypted under the RADIUS shared secret (or a per-AP key for cloud databases), together with the unit, room or user identity and the VLAN. The AP binds the client to this PSK and identity, synchronises the binding across all APs in the domain, and moves the client to the configured profile: 802.11v-capable clients via a BSS Transition Management request, and other clients by disconnection and reconnection. On that profile, SAE-capable clients complete WPA3-SAE and WPA2-only clients complete WPA2-PSK, both with the learned key and no further search, so the companion BSSID serves only for onboarding. We present the design, attribute dictionary, algorithms, complexity and security properties, together with laboratory validation on commercial access points.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据中心承担日益增长的人工智能工作负载，跨地理分布基础设施的能量感知编排变得越来越重要，但现有方法往往将自动化决策制定的能量成本排除在系统级可持续性评估之外，导致对AI原生编排的真实能耗缺乏完整认知。

Method: 1) 构建能量感知的智能体编排框架，将LLM推理作为控制循环的可测量组件；2) 在跨越两个欧洲数据中心的多站点Kubernetes测试床上进行实验，测试床从配电单元到每Pod能量归因进行多层仪器化；3) 比较三种推理策略：单轮工具调用(single-shot tool calling)、ReAct、计划-执行(plan-and-execute)；4) 使用10个具有不同决策复杂度的自然语言编排意图进行评估；5) 采用前缀缓存(prefix caching)技术优化能耗。

Result: 1) ReAct和计划-执行策略在所有评估意图上都能生成可执行的工具调用，但需要多次LLM推理；2) 单轮配置成本较低但无法产生可执行动作(non-actionable)；3) 前缀缓存将ReAct的平均GPU能耗降低了29.4%；4) 推理策略和意图复杂度强烈影响编排决策期间的GPU能耗；5) 对于评估的轻量级工作负载，一秒测量窗口内观察到的基础设施能量变化不足以抵消测量的GPU决策能量；6) 需要基于能量预算选择智能体策略以实现可持续的AI原生编排。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在评估AI原生编排可持续性时考虑推理开销的重要性，并推动了基于能量预算的智能体策略选择，为未来能量感知的自动化基础设施管理提供了实证依据和方法论指导。

Abstract: Energy-aware orchestration across geographically distributed infrastructures is increasingly important as data centers absorb growing Artificial Intelligence (AI) workloads. However, existing approaches often exclude the energy cost of automated decision-making from system-level sustainability assessments. This paper presents an energy-aware agentic orchestration framework that treats Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning as a measurable component of the control loop. We compare single-shot tool calling, ReAct, and plan-and-execute strategies on a multi-site Kubernetes testbed spanning two European data centers and instrumented at layers ranging from power distribution units to per-pod energy attribution. Using ten natural-language orchestration intents with varying decision complexity, we show that reasoning strategy and intent complexity strongly influence graphics processing unit (GPU) energy consumption during orchestration decisions. ReAct and plan-and-execute generated executable tool calls across all evaluated intents but required multiple LLM inferences, whereas the single-shot configuration was less costly but non-actionable. Prefix caching reduced mean ReAct GPU energy consumption by 29.4%. For the evaluated lightweight workload, the infrastructure-energy changes observed within the one-second measurement window were insufficient to offset the measured GPU decision energy. These findings demonstrate the importance of accounting for reasoning overhead when assessing the sustainability of AI-native orchestration and motivate energy-budgeted selection of agent strategies.

</details>


### [62] [Stackelberg Game with Multi-Agent Transformer for ISRS-Aware Resource Re-optimization in Multi-Band Optical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14830)
*Jiaxin Liu,Rentao Gu,Miao Zhu,Zeguang Zou,Hui Li,Yuefeng Ji*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对多波段光网络中ISRS导致的业务非线性耦合问题，提出SG-MAT方法，通过Stackelberg博弈的领导者-跟随者结构协调信道重分配，实现利用率提升并将阻塞率降低约30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展推动计算基础设施向互联AI数据中心演进，多波段光网络成为此类连接的骨干。为应对日益动态的流量需求，这些网络需要持续进行资源重优化以提高资源利用率并降低业务阻塞。然而，多波段传输放大了信道间受激拉曼散射(ISRS)，导致业务间产生非线性耦合。这种耦合使重优化变得复杂，因为局部调整会扰动全局功率分布，造成跨业务的相互依赖优化决策。此外，非线性交互随重优化业务子集的选择而变化，不恰当的选择可能降低重优化效果或削弱可获得的收益。

Method: 提出了一种基于Stackelberg博弈的多智能体Transformer(SG-MAT)方法用于ISRS感知的资源重优化。SG-MAT采用领导者-跟随者结构：基于MAT的跟随者建模非线性耦合并生成协调的信道重分配，将利用率改进反馈给领导者；领导者利用该反馈识别关键业务子集，逼近Stackelberg均衡以指导全局决策，实现一致且高效的重优化。

Result: 仿真结果表明，SG-MAT实现了最高的利用率改进，在中等流量负载下将阻塞率降低约30%，且计算复杂度与基准方法相当。

Conclusion: SG-MAT通过Stackelberg博弈框架有效解决了多波段光网络中ISRS引起的非线性耦合问题，在保持可接受计算复杂度的同时显著提升了资源重优化性能，为AI数据中心互联光网络的动态资源管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of generative AI is driving computing infrastructures toward interconnected AI data centers. Multi-band optical networks have become the backbone of such connectivity. To accommodate increasingly dynamic traffic demands, these networks require continual resource re-optimization to improve resource utilization and reduce service blocking. However, multi-band transmission amplifies inter-channel stimulated Raman scattering(ISRS), inducing nonlinear coupling among services. This coupling complicates re-optimization, as local adjustments perturb the global power profile, creating interdependent optimization decisions across services. Moreover, the nonlinear interactions vary with the subset of services selected for re-optimization, and inappropriate selection may reduce re-optimization effectiveness or diminish achievable benefits. To address these challenges, we propose a Stackelberg Game with Multi-Agent Transformer (SG-MAT) approach for ISRS-aware resource re-optimization. SG-MAT employs a leader-follower structure, in which the MAT-based follower models nonlinear coupling and generates coordinated channel reallocations, feeding the utilization improvements to the leader. Using this feedback, the leader identifies critical service subsets, approximating a Stackelberg equilibrium to guide global decisions and enabling consistent and efficient re-optimization. Simulation results show that SG-MAT achieves the highest utilization improvement and reduces the blocking ratio by approximately 30% under moderate traffic loads, with computational complexity comparable to benchmarks.

</details>


### [63] [Conflict-Aware Autonomous Resource Orchestration for 5G Edge Slicing Using Multi-Agent Systems and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14903)
*Nathan R. Prediger,Rodrigo Moreira,Juliano Araujo Wickboldt,Tereza C. Carvalho,Pedro Oliveira,Paulo Novais,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双时间尺度架构，将大语言模型（LLM）与多智能体拍卖机制结合，用于5G边缘网络切片资源分配：LLM在宏观层面将自然语言意图转化为经济策略参数，确定性Vickrey拍卖在操作层面解决资源竞争，并通过Kubernetes和Calico直接执行分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G边缘网络切片需要同时满足两个耦合需求：理解高层运营商意图和在动态竞争下重新分配稀缺的计算和带宽资源。现有方法存在局限：集中式优化在边缘竞争快速变化时面临计算和响应性挑战；多智能体系统（MAS）拍卖虽能支持资源仲裁，但仍依赖运营商预定义的经济参数和优先级；直接将LLM嵌入循环控制回路可能引入不可忽略的推理延迟和非确定性行为。

Method: 提出双时间尺度架构：（1）宏观尺度：基于LLM的Strategist模块将自然语言网络意图转化为经济策略参数（预算、收入和竞价激进程度）；（2）操作尺度：由SPADE-based切片代理运行的确定性、受Vickrey启发的二价机制解决周期性资源竞争；（3）执行层：通过直接修补Kubernetes CPU限制和Calico CNI带宽注解在用户面功能（UPF）Pod上强制执行分配结果。

Result: 在真实Kubernetes/Open5GS边缘集群上进行端到端评估，涵盖两个互补场景各重复30次：（1）智慧城市场景：自然语言公共安全意图在几轮拍卖内传播为可测量的切片竞价和带宽分配变化；（2）分层切片场景：机制提供优先级相关的资源和QoE差异化，适应突发需求变化，保持配置的最小保证，同时维持共享带宽池的近乎完全分配。

Conclusion: 该双时间尺度架构成功弥合了高层自然语言意图与低层确定性资源分配之间的差距，实现了意图驱动的自适应资源管理，在保证确定性和低延迟的同时，充分利用了LLM的语义理解能力和传统拍卖机制的效率优势。

Abstract: 5G edge network slicing must reconcile two coupled requirements: understanding high-level operator intent and reallocating scarce computing and bandwidth resources under dynamic contention. Centralized optimization can face computational and responsiveness challenges under rapidly changing edge contention, and Multi-Agent System (MAS)-based auctions can support resource arbitration but still depend on operator-defined economic parameters and priorities. Embedding a Large Language Model (LLM) directly in a recurring control loop, by contrast, may introduce non-negligible inference latency and non-deterministic behavior. This paper proposes a two-timescale architecture that addresses this gap: an LLM-based Strategist translates natural-language network intents into economic policy parameters (budget, income, and bidding-aggressiveness) at the macro scale, while a deterministic, Vickrey-inspired second-price mechanism run by SPADE-based slice agents resolves recurring resource contention at the operational scale, with the resulting allocations enforced by patching Kubernetes CPU limits and Calico CNI bandwidth annotations directly on User Plane Function pods. We evaluate the architecture end-to-end on a real Kubernetes/Open5GS edge cluster across two complementary scenarios, each repeated 30 times: a Smart-City scenario, in which natural-language public-safety intents propagate into measurable changes in slice bids and bandwidth allocations within a few auction rounds, and a Hierarchical-slicing scenario, in which the mechanism provides priority-dependent resource and QoE differentiation, adapts to abrupt demand changes, and preserves configured minimum guarantees while maintaining near-complete allocation of the shared bandwidth pool.

</details>


### [64] [Clock-Free Stochastic Scheduling for Real-Time Synchronized Content Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15114)
*Xuan Wang,Xiaoyi Wu,Ying Sun,Atilla Eryilmaz,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出无时钟延迟补偿框架，通过SPP-ADMM算法优化多用户内容同步交付，仅需ACK反馈，复杂度O(N log N)，收敛速率O(1/√K)，在真实和合成数据上均优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时同步内容交付对云游戏、协作混合现实和直播等应用至关重要，这些应用要求服务器确保一组用户同时感知相同内容。由于网络延迟的异构性和不可预测性，实现同步极具挑战性。现有方法通常需要同步时钟或客户端时间戳，限制了部署灵活性。

Method: 提出了一种无时钟调度框架，引入延迟补偿来控制服务器对每个用户的交付时间。将问题建模为具有非光滑目标的凸随机优化问题，采用随机邻近点（SPP）算法更新延迟补偿，仅需历史延迟样本而非延迟分布。利用子问题结构，应用ADMM实现高效更新，每次迭代复杂度至多为O(N log N)。框架还扩展至处理传输失败场景。

Result: 建立了O(1/√K)的收敛速率，其中K为内容传输次数。基于合成和真实世界延迟轨迹的仿真表明，该算法在同步性能和整体延迟成本之间取得了最优平衡，且始终优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 该框架通过仅使用确认反馈即可实现灵活部署，无需同步时钟或客户端时间戳，有效解决了异构网络环境下的内容同步交付问题，具有良好的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Real-time synchronized content delivery is critical for applications such as cloud gaming, collaborative mixed reality, and live streaming, where a server must ensure that a group of users perceives the same content simultaneously for each delivery. Due to heterogeneous and unpredictable network delays, ensuring synchronization is highly challenging. We propose a clock-free scheduling framework that introduces latency compensation to control the server's delivery time for each user, aiming to maximize synchronization performance (i.e., minimizing the gap between the maximum and minimum latencies) while maintaining a low overall latency cost. Unlike prior work requiring synchronized clocks or client-side timestamps, our approach uses only acknowledgment feedback, enabling flexible deployment across diverse environments. We formulate the problem as a convex stochastic optimization with a non-smooth objective. To solve the problem, we employ the stochastic proximal point (SPP) algorithm that updates the delay compensation, using only the previous latency samples, rather than the latency distribution. By exploiting the subproblem structure, we apply ADMM to enable efficient updates with per-iteration complexity of at most $\mathcal{O}(N\log N)$, where $N$ is the number of users. We establish a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{K})$, where $K$ is the number of content deliveries for transmission. The framework is further extended to handle transmission failures, where packet loss results in successful delivery to only a subset of users. Finally, simulations using both synthetic and real-world latency traces demonstrate the consistent superiority of our algorithm against baseline methods.

</details>


### [65] [Tutorial on Closed-Loop and Application-Aware Digital Twins for Future Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15120)
*Yuchen Liu,Sara Garcia Sanchez,Mingzhe Chen,Xingqin Lin,Berk Canberk,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 数字孪生网络（DTN）是实现下一代无线网络从传统通信向闭环智能系统转型的关键技术。本文通过教程式综述，系统性地介绍了DTN的设计原则、资源优化、物理层技术、闭环管理以及标准化进展，为DTN的研究与应用提供了全面指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持扩展现实、自主系统和大规模网络物理基础设施等新兴应用，下一代无线网络需要从传统通信范式向紧密集成的闭环系统演进。数字孪生网络（DTN）通过实时双向交互和自适应控制机制连接物理世界与数字世界，成为这一转型的关键使能技术。然而，目前缺乏对DTN设计与优化的全面系统性综述。

Method: 采用教程式综述方法，系统性地从多个维度分析DTN：1）DTN创建的基本设计原则（高保真孪生建模、遥测集成、网络物理同步）；2）资源优化与语义驱动DTN（应用感知目标、语义抽象）；3）物理层优化技术（联合感知通信、自适应无线资源控制）；4）DTN引导的网络管理与反馈优化（闭环学习、实时控制、强化驱动自适应）；5）标准化工作与使能工具。

Result: 建立了DTN的统一框架，明确了其在应用感知、AI原生、闭环无线系统中的核心地位；梳理了从物理层到应用层的多层次优化策略；识别了DTN在实际部署中的关键挑战和未来研究方向，包括标准化需求和使能工具的发展。

Conclusion: 本文全面综述了数字孪生网络（DTN）的设计与优化，涵盖高保真孪生建模、资源优化、语义驱动、物理层优化、闭环网络管理等多个维度，并讨论了标准化进展和实际部署工具，为DTN作为应用感知、AI原生、闭环无线系统的新兴范式提供了统一视角。

Abstract: To support emerging applications such as extended reality, autonomous systems, and large-scale cyber-physical infrastructures, next-generation wireless networks must evolve beyond traditional communication paradigms toward tightly integrated and closed-loop systems. Digital Twin Networks (DTNs) have recently emerged as a key enabler of this transformation by bridging the physical and digital worlds through real-time, bidirectional interactions and adaptive control mechanisms. In this tutorial, we provide a comprehensive overview of DTN design and optimization across multiple system dimensions. Specifically, we first introduce the fundamental design principles of DTN creation, including high-fidelity twin modeling, telemetry integration, and cyber-physical synchronization. We then present resource optimization and semantic-driven DTNs, where application-aware objectives and semantic abstractions guide efficient data processing, communication, and decision-making. Subsequently, we explore physical-layer optimization techniques for DTNs, emphasizing joint sensing, communication, and adaptive wireless resource control. Furthermore, we examine DTN-guided network management and feedback optimization, focusing on closed-loop learning, real-time control, and reinforcement-driven adaptation between digital twins and physical networks. Finally, we review ongoing standardization efforts and enabling tools that support the practical deployment of DTNs in real-world systems. This tutorial aims to provide a unified perspective on DTNs as an emerging paradigm for application-aware, AI-native, and closed-loop wireless systems, and to highlight key challenges and future research directions in this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [66] [NAS Timers for Non-Terrestrial Networks: Current Standards and Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15211)
*Cheng Liu,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文分析了5G NAS响应监督定时器在非地面网络（NTN）中的适配问题，指出当前3GPP基于卫星RAT类型选择定时器值的粒度不足，提出从配置粒度和信息范围两个维度优化定时器配置，并通过注册流程案例说明了避免过早超时与及时恢复之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非地面网络中，5G NAS响应监督定时器需要考虑卫星传播、地面传输和多核心网功能处理等延迟因素。然而，当前3GPP规范仅根据卫星NG-RAN无线接入技术（RAT）类型选择定时器值，无法反映同一低轨（LEO）网络内不同控制路径在传播延迟和处理负载上的差异，可能导致定时器过早超时或消息丢失恢复不及时的问题。

Method: 本文采用规范分析与案例研究相结合的方法：（1）回顾相关3GPP定时器语义；（2）追踪5G注册流程中不同定时器所覆盖的延迟组件；（3）沿配置粒度（granularity）和信息范围（information scope）两个维度组织候选改进方案；（4）通过注册案例研究分析避免过早定时器超时与及时从消息丢失中恢复之间的权衡。

Result: 研究识别了当前定时器配置的局限性，系统梳理了候选优化方案的两个维度，并通过案例分析量化了定时器配置的关键权衡。最终明确了未来规范需要解决的信息需求和协调机制，以支持可互操作的定时器配置。

Conclusion: 为支持NTN中可互操作的定时器配置，未来3GPP规范需要：扩展定时器配置的粒度（超越RAT级别到路径或链路级别）、明确定时器所需的信息范围（包括传播延迟、处理延迟和网络负载等）、以及建立UE与网络侧之间的协调机制，从而在避免伪超时和保证快速故障恢复之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Response-supervision timers in the 5G Non-Access Stratum (NAS) bound the time allowed for signaling exchanges before retransmission or abnormal-case handling begins. In non-terrestrial networks, that interval can include satellite propagation, terrestrial transport, and processing by multiple core-network functions. Under current 3GPP specifications, the applicable timer value for satellite access is selected according to the satellite NG-RAN radio access technology (RAT) type. However, control paths within the same low Earth orbit (LEO) network can differ in propagation delay and processing load. This article reviews the relevant 3GPP timer semantics and traces the delay components covered by different timers through 5G registration. It organizes candidate refinements along two dimensions: configuration granularity and information scope. A registration case study illustrates the trade-off between avoiding premature timer expiry and recovering promptly from message loss. The article identifies the information and coordination that future specifications would need to address to support interoperable timer configuration.

</details>


### [67] [Cost-Aware Conflict Triage for xApp Control Conflicts in Open RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15310)
*Abdul Wadud,Fatemeh Golpayegani,Nima Afraz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种成本感知的Open RAN冲突分类框架，通过ECDF评估严重程度并结合成本效益分析，在事件级别智能选择容忍、延迟或缓解动作，相比无脑缓解基线将净效用提升3.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Open RAN支持多厂商部署，多个xApp独立优化移动性、负载、覆盖和能耗等RAN控制参数。当xApp作用于重叠的控制参数时，其独立的控制回路会产生冲突决策，导致网络KPI下降。现有冲突评估方法只能估计严重程度，但无法判断干预是否具有成本效益（cost-justified）。

Method: 提出一种成本感知的冲突分类（triage）框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）基于ECDF（经验累积分布函数）的严重程度评估；（2）事件级决策层。具体流程为：将检测到的冲突记录分组为episode，提取9个特征，估计每种机制的成本与收益，最终分配三种动作之一：容忍（tolerate）、延迟（defer）或缓解（mitigate）。

Result: 在仿真驱动的Open RAN环境中，通过30次蒙特卡洛运行、4个并发xApp进行评估。框架对检测到的episode分配比例为：延迟66.8%、缓解19.8%、容忍13.4%。平均每次干预的净效用（net utility）为0.132，比始终缓解（always-mitigate）基线高3.9倍，通过成本感知的选择性避免了低价值干预。

Conclusion: 该框架通过引入成本效益分析，将冲突管理从单纯 severity 评估提升到经济理性决策层面，显著提升了干预的净效用，证明了在Open RAN多xApp场景中进行选择性冲突管理的价值。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Network (Open RAN) enables multi-vendor deployments where concurrent RAN control applications (xApps) independently optimise mobility, load, coverage, and energy within the Near Real Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT RIC). When xApps act on overlapping control parameters, their isolated loops produce conflicting decisions that degrade the network Key Performance Indicators (KPIs). Existing conflict-evaluation methods estimate severity but do not answer whether the intervention is cost-justified. This paper proposes a cost-aware conflict triage framework that pairs ECDF-based severity evaluation with an event-level decision layer. The framework groups detected conflict records into episodes, extracts nine features, estimates per-mechanism costs and benefits, and assigns one of three actions: tolerate, defer, or mitigate. Evaluated in a simulator-driven Open RAN setting over 30 Monte Carlo runs with four concurrent xApps, the framework defers 66.8 %, mitigates 19.8 %, and tolerates 13.4 % of detected episodes, achieving a mean net utility of 0.132 per intervention, 3.9x higher than an always-mitigate baseline, by avoiding low- value interventions through cost-aware selectivity.

</details>


### [68] [The Evolution of In-Premise Networks In the Era of Immersive Services: From Copper to Fiber-to-The Room [Invited Tutorial]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15566)
*Elaine Wong,Sourav Mondal*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对沉浸式应用（特别是协同扩展现实XR）对网络提出的严苛需求，提出了一种融合粗粒度流量预测与智能室内漫游的新型资源分配框架，用于优化光纤到户（FTTH）+光纤到房间（FTTR）+WiFi的多层网络架构。数值评估表明，长时域多步流量预测可显著降低推理频率，实现跨房间的无缝移动性管理，有效满足协同XR的QoE需求，并探讨了通过多元化服务摊薄FTTR资本支出的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信行业正迈向以体验为中心的时代，沉浸式应用（如协同XR）对网络提出严苛需求。传统PON-based FTTH架构虽能提供高容量宽带，但接入网与室内网络之间的性能差距日益扩大，需要将光纤进一步延伸至室内。FTTR作为补充现有铜缆和WiFi技术的关键使能技术正在兴起，亟需研究其架构转型的驱动因素及网络优化方案。

Method: 本文提出了一种新型资源分配框架，核心包括：（1）粗粒度流量预测（coarse-grained traffic forecasting）：实现长时域、多步提前的流量预测；（2）智能室内漫游（intelligent in-premise roaming）：实现网络编排的无缝跨房间移动性管理；（3）分层AI模型部署：根据FTTH、FTTR和WiFi各段的计算能力灵活部署人工智能模型；（4）数值评估：在代表性多层网络架构上验证框架性能。

Result: 数值评估表明：（1）长时域、多步提前流量预测显著降低了推理频率；（2）实现了协调化的资源分配和网络编排的无缝跨房间移动性；（3）有效满足了协同XR的QoE需求；（4）通过支持统一物理层上的多样化服务，FTTR能够提升基础设施利用率、开辟新收入来源、最大化室内光纤部署价值，从而摊薄资本支出。

Conclusion: FTTR是弥合接入网与室内网络性能差距、支撑沉浸式体验驱动应用的关键架构演进。本文提出的融合流量预测与智能漫游的资源分配框架，通过分层AI部署和协调化网络管理，能够有效满足协同XR的严苛QoE需求。未来应进一步探索FTTR基础设施的多业务承载能力，以提升投资回报率并推动FTTR的规模化部署。

Abstract: The telecommunications industry is shifting toward an experience-driven era defined by immersive applications that impose stringent network demands. While passive optical network (PON)-based Fiber-To-The-Home (FTTH) architectures have historically delivered high-capacity broadband, a growing performance gap between access and indoor networks necessitates driving fiber deeper into premises. Complementing existing copper and WiFi technologies, Fiber-to-the-Room (FTTR) is emerging as a critical enabler. This article examines the drivers of this architectural shift, specifically focusing on collaborative extended reality (XR). We analyze the multi-modal traffic characteristics and Quality-of-Experience (QoE) requirements of XR, alongside the resulting network performance implications. To address these demands, we propose a novel resource allocation framework integrating coarse-grained traffic forecasting with intelligent in-premise roaming. This framework facilitates the flexible deployment of artificial intelligence models tailored to the computational capabilities of the FTTH, FTTR, and WiFi segments. Numerical evaluations of a representative multi-tier network demonstrate that long-horizon, multi-step-ahead traffic forecasts significantly reduce inference frequency. This approach enables coordinated resource allocation and network-orchestrated seamless mobility across rooms, effectively satisfying collaborative XR QoE requirements. Finally, we highlight strategies to amortize FTTR capital expenditure by extending the optical infrastructure's role beyond basic connectivity. By supporting diverse services over a unified physical layer, FTTR enhances infrastructure utilization, unlocks novel revenue streams, and maximizes the value of in-premise fiber deployments.

</details>


### [69] [Coarse-Grained Predictive Resource Allocation Framework enabling In-Premise XR Collaborations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15581)
*Sourav Mondal,Elaine Wong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出分层AI驱动的流量预测框架：FTTR段用TCN-BiLSTM、FTTH段用Transformer模型预测XR流量，实现主动资源分配与无缝漫游，Transformer预测精度提升约75%，显著改善XR体验质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式扩展现实（XR）应用是下一代网络中以人为本连接的关键使能技术。然而，在满足其高吞吐量、超低延迟和高可靠性需求的同时防止网络晕动症仍然是一个关键挑战，尤其是在无线连接非确定性的环境中。光纤到房间（FTTR）作为已广泛部署的光纤到户（FTTH）基础设施的延伸，提供了一个有前景的解决方案。但满足XR协作者的严格体验质量（QoE）需求需要在FTTH、FTTR和WiFi多层级联段之间进行稳健的资源分配。

Method: 本文提出了一种粗粒度流量预测赋能的主动资源分配和智能室内漫游框架。该框架根据各网络段的计算能力扩展人工智能（AI）模型复杂度：（1）在FTTR段，采用混合时序卷积和双向长短期记忆网络（TCN-BiLSTM）进行多模态XR流量预测，集成无源光网络（PON）和具备多链路操作（MLO）及正交频分多址（OFDMA）的WiFi接入点；（2）在FTTH段，部署基于Transformer的时间序列模型（如Informer、Autoformer、PatchTST和TFT）预测FTTR段聚合的复杂XR流量以进行回传传输。

Result: 评估表明，基于Transformer的模型在长期预测方面比混合模型准确度高约75%。大量仿真证实，所提出的框架在满足XR QoE需求方面显著优于传统方法。长期预测降低了推理频率，实现了协调的FTTR-WiFi资源分配和跨房间的无缝网络编排移动性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过分层AI驱动的流量预测与资源分配框架，有效解决了XR应用在多层级网络中的QoE保障问题，证明了在不同网络层级采用适配复杂度的AI模型进行长期流量预测对优化XR体验的有效性。

Abstract: Immersive extended reality (XR) applications are key enablers of human-centric connectivity in next-generation networks. However, meeting their high-throughput, ultra-low-latency, and high-reliability demands while preventing cyber-sickness remains a critical challenge, especially in environments with non-deterministic wireless connectivity. Fiber-To-The-Room (FTTR), extending widely deployed Fiber-To-The-Home (FTTH) infrastructure, offers a promising solution. Yet, satisfying the stringent Quality-of-Experience (QoE) requirements of XR collaborators demands robust resource allocation across the multi-tier cascade of FTTH, FTTR, and WiFi segments. In this paper, we propose a coarse-grained traffic forecasting-empowered proactive resource allocation and intelligent in-premise roaming framework. Here, artificial intelligence (AI) model complexities are scaled to the computational capabilities of each network segment. At the FTTR segment, we employ a hybrid temporal convolutional and bi-directional long short-term memory (TCN-BiLSTM) network for multi-modal XR traffic forecasting, integrating passive optical network (PON) and WiFi access points with multi-link operation (MLO) and orthogonal frequency-division multiple access (OFDMA). The resulting long-horizon forecasts reduce inference frequency, enabling coordinated FTTR-WiFi resource allocation and seamless network-orchestrated mobility across rooms. At the FTTH segment, we deploy Transformer-based time-series models (e.g., Informer, Autoformer, PatchTST, and TFT) to predict the complex XR traffic aggregated in FTTR segments for transmission over the FTTH backhaul. Evaluations show Transformer-based models are up to $\sim$75% more accurate in long-horizon forecasting than the hybrid model. Extensive simulations confirm the proposed framework significantly outperforms traditional methods in satisfying XR QoE requirements.

</details>


### [70] [NetRL: Powering Network Decisions via NetGRPO-Driven Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16226)
*Lingfeng Peng,Xiaohui Xie,Yang Bao,Yong Cui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NetRL是一个基于大语言模型的两阶段强化学习框架，通过监督微调和在线策略优化解决网络决策任务（如自适应码率、负载均衡、拥塞控制）。核心组件NetGRPO通过多轨迹评估、置信度感知预算分配和稳定的策略更新机制，在QoE、作业完成时间和网络效用等指标上显著优于现有方法，同时训练时间减少27.1%且具备良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络决策任务（自适应码率流媒体、负载均衡、拥塞控制等）需要策略处理异构状态和动作，同时平衡近期与长期后果。现有方案存在三个关键缺陷：(1)跨网络任务泛化能力差，需要大量任务特定的重新设计；(2)缺乏对长期动作后果的可靠评估；(3)基于在线交互的学习方法面临优化不稳定问题。

Method: 提出NetRL框架，采用两阶段训练范式：第一阶段在演示轨迹上进行监督微调，第二阶段进行在线策略优化。技术贡献包括：(1)通用混合嵌入接口——融合文本任务描述与精确数值状态；(2)网络动作头——生成有效决策；(3)核心算法NetGRPO——通过多未来轨迹评估候选动作，利用置信度感知预算将搜索深度导向不确定替代方案，并执行组相对、裁剪和KL正则化更新。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1)相比NetLLM，QoE提升23.6%；(2)相比Genet，作业完成时间降低12.2%；(3)相比Orca，网络效用提升24.1%，在评估方法中达到最优性能；(4)置信度感知预算分配使端到端训练时间减少27.1%，且不降低验证QoE；(5)NetRL收敛稳定，无需重新训练即可泛化到未见网络轨迹。代码和模型权重已开源。

Conclusion: NetRL通过将大语言模型的语义理解能力与强化学习的序列决策能力相结合，为网络决策任务提供了一个通用、稳定且高效的解决方案。NetGRPO的置信度感知多轨迹评估机制和正则化更新策略有效解决了长期后果评估和优化稳定性问题，证明了LLM-RL混合架构在网络领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Network decision-making tasks, including adaptive bitrate streaming, load balancing, and congestion control, require
  policies to process heterogeneous states and actions while balancing near- and long-term consequences. Existing solutions
  generalize poorly across network tasks, require substantial task-specific redesign, and lack reliable evaluation of long-
  horizon action consequences. Methods that learn through online interaction additionally face unstable optimization. We
  present NetRL, a two-stage LLM-based reinforcement learning framework that combines supervised fine-tuning on
  demonstration trajectories with online policy optimization. A universal mixed-embedding interface combines textual task
  descriptions with precise numerical states, while a network action head produces valid decisions. At its core, NetGRPO
  evaluates candidate actions over multiple future trajectories, directs deeper search toward uncertain alternatives
  through confidence-aware budgeting, and performs group-relative, clipped, and KL-regularized updates. NetRL achieves
  23.6% higher QoE than NetLLM, 12.2% lower job completion time than Genet, and 24.1% higher network utility than Orca,
  yielding state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods. Confidence-aware budgeting reduces end-to-end training
  time by 27.1% without reducing validation QoE. NetRL also converges stably and generalizes to unseen network traces
  without retraining. Code and model weights are available at the repository linked in this paper.

</details>


### [71] [Island-Ready Communication Networks: Connectivity Monitoring and State Synchronization for 5G Crisis Connectivity at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16284)
*Leon Janzen,Maria Koch,Matthias Hollick*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对危机场景下5G网络孤岛部署中的状态同步和连接监控问题提出解决方案，通过开源项目集成和国家级网络仿真验证，发现切换延迟主要来自接入网重配置，且50个核心网部署下同步峰值速率达9.36MB/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤岛就绪的5G部署能够在与全球互联网断开连接时切换到孤岛运行模式，为隔离区域提供边缘连接能力。然而，状态同步和连接监控等关键技术挑战阻碍了孤岛就绪5G网络的实际部署和应用，亟需解决这些开放性的研究问题。

Method: 本研究聚焦于实现孤岛就绪所需的状态同步和连接监控功能的设计与实现。作为概念验证和性能评估，研究团队将解决方案集成到开源项目中，并基于真实蜂窝流量数据仿真了一个全国范围的5G通信网络，通过该实验平台对切换延迟和同步开销进行系统评估。

Result: 评估研究表明：(1) 向孤岛运行模式切换的延迟主要来源于接入网重配置过程；(2) 在高度分布式的核心网络中，同步开销随5G核心网数量呈超线性增长趋势；(3) 在50个核心网部署规模下，同步数据速率的峰值达到9.36MB/s。

Conclusion: 本工作为5G孤岛就绪部署的实际实现提供了关键的技术解决方案，特别是在状态同步和连接监控方面。研究结果揭示了接入网重配置是切换延迟的主要瓶颈，以及分布式核心网同步开销的可扩展性挑战，为未来优化5G网络在危机场景下的孤岛运行能力提供了重要的设计参考和性能基准。

Abstract: Island-ready 5G deployments provide edge connectivity for isolated regions in crisis scenarios by switching to island operation when they are cut off from the global Internet. This work addresses open research challenges that hinder the practical realization of island readiness, with a focus on the required state synchronization and connectivity monitoring functionality. As a proof of concept and for performance evaluation, we integrate our solutions in open-source projects and emulate a country-wide 5G communication network with real-world cellular traffic. Our evaluation studies the switching latency of transitions to island operation, showing that most of the transition delay stems from the access network reconfiguration. We study the synchronization overhead of a highly distributed core network, finding that the synchronization traffic grows faster than linearly with the number of 5G cores, reaching peak synchronization data rates of 9.36MB/s for a deployment of 50 cores.

</details>


### [72] [CALIPER: Calibrating HPC/AI Network Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16477)
*Seyyidahmed Lahmer,Daniel Seibel,Srijeet Basu,Arno Troch,Jayesh Badwaik,Subhomoy Bhattacharyya,Abubakr Nada,Debjyoti Bhattacharjee,Vinay Kumar Baapanapalli Yadaiah,Michael Peeters,Andreas Herten,Darayus Adil Patel*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CALIPER是一种面向HPC/AI网络模拟器的分层约束校准方法论，通过区分可观测参数与辅助参数并对后者施加可行域约束，实现了在64节点系统上平均误差小于5%的高精度性能预测，且预测能力可泛化至训练数据未覆盖的规模范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络模拟在HPC/AI系统研究中具有重要作用，但现有的网络模拟器校准和验证方法缺乏一种系统化的方法论，无法有效结合参数对齐、约束推理和独立验证，特别是在以集合通信为中心的HPC/AI网络场景下。因此，需要一种能够区分直接可观测参数与辅助参数，并对后者进行合理约束的校准方法。

Method: 本文提出了CALIPER方法论，用于HPC/AI网络模拟器的系统化校准与验证。CALIPER采用分层、可感知规模的校准策略，将直接可观测的系统参数与需要通过校准配置的辅助参数区分开来，并将辅助参数约束在与目标系统一致的可行域内。该方法通过约束推理确保参数的可识别性和泛化能力。

Result: 在Jülich超级计算中心多达64节点的参考系统上，使用集合通信基准测试对CALIPER进行评估。验证结果表明，CALIPER识别的校准参数配置在scale-up和scale-out网络域中预测系统性能的平均误差小于5%。实验证明，分层约束校准产生的模拟器配置其预测精度能够持续超出用于拟合的观测数据范围。

Conclusion: CALIPER证明了分层、约束校准方法能够生成具有持久预测精度的模拟器配置，有效解决了HPC/AI网络模拟器校准中的参数可识别性和泛化性问题，为大规模网络系统研究提供了可靠的模拟基础。

Abstract: Network simulation plays an important role in HPC/AI system research by enabling exploration of network architectures, communication strategies, and system configurations at scales that may be impractical to study experimentally. Existing approaches to network simulator calibration and validation lack a principled methodology that combines parameter alignment, constrained inference, and independent validation for collective-centric HPC/AI networks. We present CALIPER, a methodology for systematic calibration and validation of HPC/AI network simulators. CALIPER distinguishes directly observable system parameters from auxiliary parameters which are configured via calibration and constrains the latter to feasible domains consistent with the target system. We evaluate CALIPER's hierarchical, scale-aware calibration and its effects on parameter identifiability and generalization using collective communication benchmarks on a reference system with up to 64-nodes at the Jülich Supercomputing Centre. Validation results show that the calibrated parameter configurations identified by CALIPER predict system performance with less than 5% mean errors across scale-up and scale-out network domains. CALIPER demonstrates that hierarchical, constrained calibration can produce simulator configurations whose predictive accuracy persists beyond the observations used for fitting.

</details>


### [73] [Offline-Tuned Service-Aware TN-NTN Feeder Allocation for 6G Post-Disaster Service Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16501)
*Donglin Wang,Anjie Qiu,Qiuheng Zhou,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交叉熵方法（CEM）的离线参数调优策略，用于灾后场景下非地面网络（NTN）馈线容量受限时的服务优先级感知编排，以提升地面网络故障时的应急服务连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害期间地面网络（TN）故障会导致幸存基站过载并中断应急服务；非地面网络（NTN）虽可提供备用回传，但当用户迁移时其馈线链路可能成为瓶颈。因此，需要在灾后快照场景下研究网络韧性，解决受限NTN馈线容量的优化编排问题。

Method: 提出一种轻量级交叉熵方法（CEM），离线调优馈线权重、卸载参数和用户排序参数（非在线AI控制器）；构建Python系统级仿真器，基于抽象的TN-NTN链路和排队模型扫描不同运行区域；采用服务优先级感知机制区分应急业务与普通业务。

Result: 性能增益取决于故障严重程度：在180 Mbps馈线速率且TN完全中断场景下，所提策略将应急加权连续性指标C_emg从0.399提升至0.666（绝对增益0.267；95% Bootstrap置信区间[0.264, 0.271]）；轻度损伤可能降低总体连续性。离线CEM馈线权重调优在严重故障下优于固定服务优先级，而调优的卸载和用户排序未带来可检测的增量增益。

Conclusion: 离线CEM参数调优可有效提升灾后严重故障场景下的应急服务连续性，其中馈线权重调优是关键贡献因素，而卸载和用户排序参数的优化作用有限。该方法为NTN馈线容量受限时的灾后网络韧性提供了一种轻量级、可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Terrestrial network (TN) failures during disasters can overload surviving base stations (BSs) and disrupt emergency services. A non-terrestrial network (NTN) provides fallback, but its feeder link can become a bottleneck as users migrate. This paper studies post-disaster network resilience through service-priority-aware orchestration of constrained NTN feeder capacity in a post-failure snapshot. A lightweight Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) tunes feeder, offload, and user-ranking parameters offline, not as an online artificial intelligence (AI) controller. A Python system-level simulator sweeps operating regions under abstract TN-NTN link and queueing models. Gains depend on failure severity. At a 180-Mbps feeder under total TN outage, the proposed policy increases the absolute emergency-weighted continuity metric Cemg from 0.399 to 0.666 relative to the matched rule baseline with uniform feeder scaling (paired gain: 0.267; 95% bootstrap CI: [0.264, 0.271]); mild damage can reduce aggregate continuity. Offline CEM feeder-weight tuning further improves severe-failure continuity over fixed service priorities, whereas tuned offload and user ranking provide no detectable incremental gain.

</details>


### [74] [MCCL: Measurement-based Collective Communication Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17091)
*Michele Ferrero,Gabriele Castellano,Paolo Medagliani,Sebastien Martin,Roberto Morabito,Massimo Gallo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MCCL是一个闭环集合通信库，通过被动测量GPU间吞吐量、检测灰色故障并动态热切换调度，在几乎零开销下将灰色故障时的训练时间缩短最多4.99倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式训练中，集合通信是关键性能瓶颈。现有库和合成框架采用开环调度，假设静态拓扑和峰值链路容量。然而在实际生产环境中，持续性灰色故障会静默降低链路吞吐量和延迟数小时，导致预计算调度在执行过程中效率大幅下降。

Method: 本文提出基于测量的集合通信库（MCCL），实现运行时网络测量与动态调度合成之间的闭环。MCCL被动测量GPU到GPU吞吐量，检测持续性性能退化，使用热启动合成器重新合成受影响的通信密集型调度，并在不中断训练的情况下热切换调度。

Result: 在多节点硬件原型上验证了完整闭环，并通过仿真评估集群规模场景。实验表明：MCCL开销可忽略（<2%），测量精度高（误差<1.2%），调度热切换时间低（约12ms）。在灰色故障下，MCCL在Llama和DeepSeek等先进LLM的模拟训练工作负载上，相比静态调度最多可减少4.99倍训练时间。

Conclusion: MCCL通过运行时测量与动态调度合成的闭环机制，有效解决了灰色故障导致的集合通信效率下降问题，在保证低开销和高精度的同时，显著提升了大规模分布式训练在故障场景下的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Collective communication is a critical performance bottleneck in large-scale distributed training. Existing libraries and synthesis frameworks compute open-loop schedules once, assuming a static topology and peak link capacities. In production, however, persistent gray failures can silently degrade link throughput and latency for hours, causing pre-computed schedules to lose much of their efficiency during execution. In this paper, we present Measurement-based Collective Communication Library (MCCL), a collective communication library that closes the loop between runtime network measurement and dynamic schedule synthesis. MCCL passively measures GPU-to-GPU throughput, detects persistent degradation, re-synthesizes the affected communication-intensive schedules with warm-started synthesizers, and hot-swaps them without interrupting training. We validate the complete loop on a multi-node hardware prototype and use simulations to evaluate cluster-scale scenarios. In our experiments, MCCL incurs negligible overhead (<2%) while maintaining high measurement accuracy (<1.2% error) and low schedule hot-swap time (about 12 ms). Notably, under gray failures, MCCL reduces training time by up to 4.99x over static schedules on simulated training workloads for state-of-the-art LLMs such as Llama and DeepSeek.

</details>


### [75] [Computing All-Pairs Shortest Communication Time Paths for Periodically Varying Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17372)
*Koushik Sinha,Dibakar Saha,Sai Puppala,Bhabani P. Sinha*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对LEO卫星星座动态拓扑，提出$O(M^2n^3)$复杂度的全源最短路径算法，其类矩阵乘法结构适合GPU并行化，显著加速时变网络路由计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨(LEO)卫星星座具有动态拓扑特性，传统最短路径算法难以高效处理时变网络中的全源最短路径问题。现有算法在计算效率和并行化能力方面存在不足，无法满足快速变化的LEO卫星星座中全网消息路由的实时性需求。

Method: 将时变网络建模为时间图$G=(V,E_t)$，其中$V$为$n$个顶点集合，$E_t$为在总时间窗口$T=M\tau$（$\tau$为每个时间间隔长度）内的$M$条时变边集合。设计了一种计算结构类似于两个矩阵乘法步骤的算法——先向前方向计算，再向后方向计算，以$O(M^2n^3)$时间复杂度求解全源最短路径。

Result: 所提算法实现了$O(M^2n^3)$时间复杂度的全源最短路径计算。算法的核心优势在于其计算结构天然适合在SIMD架构（如GPU）上并行化，相比传统最短路径算法可获得显著加速，能够有效支持快速变化的LEO卫星星座中的高效全网消息路由。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种面向时变网络（特别是LEO卫星星座）的高效全源最短路径算法，通过类矩阵乘法的双向计算结构实现了良好的并行化特性，为动态拓扑网络中的实时路由提供了可行的计算框架。

Abstract: We address the problem of designing an efficient algorithm for computing the all-pairs shortest-time paths in time-varying networks, specifically targeting the dynamic topological characteristics of low earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations. We model such temporal graphs as $G = (V, E_t)$, where $V=\{1, 2,\ldots , n\}$ is the set of $n$ vertices in the graph, and $E_t$ is the set of $M$ time-varying edges considered over a total time window of size $T = Mτ$, $τ$ being the duration of each interval in that time window. For such a network, our algorithm finds the all-pair shortest paths in $O(M^2n^3)$-time. A key advantage of our approach is its computational structure, which mirrors two matrix multiplication steps, first in the forward direction and then in the backward direction. This structure of the algorithm makes it exceptionally suitable for parallelization on SIMD architectures like GPUs, thereby offering a significant speedup over traditional shortest-path algorithms and enabling efficient, network-wide message routing in rapidly changing LEO satellite constellations.

</details>


### [76] [What Is the Cost of a Handover Interruption? Rethinking 5G Mobility for Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17380)
*André Melo,Yueqi Chen,Igor Kadota,Lourenço Alves Pereira*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知移动性(RAM)，通过将预测的通信中断映射到操作风险代理指标来优化5G低空航空网络的移动性决策，在圣保罗188个5G扇区的部署中实现操作风险降低45%，推动5G移动性管理从性能优化向风险管理范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空航空正朝着大规模运营方向发展，越来越依赖5G蜂窝网络连接。然而，5G移动性管理对其决策的操作风险是"盲目"的：同样的通信中断在空旷田野巡航时可能无害，但在障碍物、人群或其他飞行器附近则可能至关重要。尽管5G已提供获取位置、人口密度和无线条件的机制，但移动性决策并未将这些信息转化为操作风险指标。

Method: 本文提出风险感知移动性(Risk-Aware Mobility, RAM)，该方法利用信息陈旧度、位置不确定性和操作上下文，将预测的通信中断映射到操作风险代理指标，并将此风险暴露给现有的移动性机制。研究在包含188个授权5G扇区的圣保罗密集部署环境中进行评估。

Result: 在圣保罗密集部署环境中，风险感知切换时序相对于传统A3事件，将平均操作风险代理指标降低了45%，同时没有降低服务链路质量。这一结果表明，将5G移动性从优化切换性能转向管理移动性决策的操作风险具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 研究结果推动5G移动性管理从优化切换性能向管理移动性决策的操作风险转变。除切换时序外，该视角还提出了一个更广泛的问题：当连接性由操作风险而非仅由无线性能评估时，哪些小区应该为空中运营提供服务？

Abstract: Low-altitude aviation is moving toward large-scale operations increasingly dependent on 5G cellular connectivity. However, 5G mobility remains blind to the operational risk of its decisions: the same interruption may be harmless in cruise over an empty field but critical near obstacles, crowds or other aircraft. Although 5G already provides mechanisms to obtain location, population density, and radio conditions, mobility decisions do not translate this information into an operational risk metric. We propose Risk-Aware Mobility (RAM), which maps predicted communication disruptions to an operational-risk proxy using information staleness, position uncertainty, and operational context, and exposes this risk to existing mobility mechanisms. In a dense São Paulo deployment with 188 licensed 5G sectors, risk-aware handover timing reduces the mean operational-risk proxy by 45\% relative to conventional A3 without degrading serving-link quality. These results motivate shifting 5G mobility from optimizing handover performance to managing the operational risk of mobility decisions. Beyond handover timing, this perspective raises a broader question: which cells should serve aerial operations when connectivity is evaluated by operational risk rather than radio performance alone?

</details>


### [77] [QoE-Aware Service Function Chain Orchestration in Space-Air-Ground Integrated Networks for In-Flight Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17851)
*Feng Liu,Bing Du,Ziyang Guo,Xiaoqi Zhang,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出融合卫星、航空和地面网络的天地空一体化架构，通过定制MADDPG算法优化服务功能链部署，实现异构航空互联网的高效QoE保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程地区缺乏地面基础设施，航空用户对与地面蜂窝网络相当的互联网服务需求日益增长，亟需构建天地空一体化网络以支持异构航空互联网服务。

Method: 提出天地空一体化网络（SAGIN）架构，融合地面网络、民航网络和近地轨道（LEO）卫星；基于真实北大西洋航班数据构建异构航空互联网；采用可重构时间扩展图（RTEG）建模SAGIN动态连接性；利用网络功能虚拟化（NFV）将数据交付建模为跨地面站、中继飞机和卫星的服务功能链（SFC）部署问题；设计融合LCSS路径过滤和softmax动作选择的定制多智能体深度确定性策略梯度（MADDPG）算法求解长期QoE最大化问题。

Result: 所提方法能够为异构飞机提供无缝连接，在QoE方面持续优于博弈论和MATD3方法。

Conclusion: 基于MADDPG的天地空一体化网络SFC部署方案可有效解决动态拓扑下的航空互联网资源优化问题，显著提升异构航空用户的服务体验质量。

Abstract: In remote regions lacking terrestrial infrastructure, aviation users increasingly demand Internet services comparable to ground cellular networks. This paper proposes a space-air-ground integrated network (SAGIN) architecture that combines ground networks, civil aviation networks, and low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites. Based on real North Atlantic flight data, a heterogeneous aviation Internet is constructed to support diverse aircraft service requirements.
  A composite Quality of Experience (QoE) metric, characterized by transmission delay, jitter, and content similarity, guides efficient resource utilization. The dynamic connectivity of SAGIN is modeled using a reconfigurable time-expanded graph (RTEG). With Network Function Virtualization (NFV), data delivery is formulated as a Service Function Chain (SFC) deployment across ground stations, relay aircraft, and satellites, resulting in a long-term QoE maximization problem.
  A tailored Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) algorithm incorporating LCSS-based path filtering and softmax-based action selection is developed. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach enables seamless connectivity for heterogeneous aircraft and consistently outperforms game-theoretic and MATD3 methods in terms of QoE.

</details>


### [78] [Towards Virtual Networking for a Time-Triggered Hypervisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17899)
*Jan Ruh,Silviu S. Craciunas,Jan Korecky*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于VirtIO的虚拟网络方案，在ACRN hypervisor中实现了符合IEEE 802.1Qbv标准的准兼容虚拟机间通信，为时间触发虚拟机之间的关键消息交换提供可预测时序，并通过实验验证了在负载网络中关键流量的端到端延迟可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟化技术通过将混合关键性应用隔离在独立虚拟机中实现共享硬件运行，但为虚拟CPU上托管的关键任务提供端到端时序保证且避免静态分区是一个难题。现有方案缺乏将计算、网络和虚拟化层统一纳入设计时全局调度的解决方案，无法跨主机和虚拟机提供端到端时序保证。

Method: 1) 提出基于VirtIO的虚拟网络架构，实现IEEE 802.1Qbv准兼容的虚拟机间通信；2) 推导所提出解决方案特性产生的网络级调度约束；3) 在ACRN hypervisor中实现该时间触发VirtIO-net原型；4) 构建包含计算、网络和虚拟化层的全局调度框架，使时间触发hypervisor按照预定全局调度表将虚拟CPU分派到处理器核心。

Result: 在负载网络环境中实现了关键流量的可预测端到端延迟。实验评估表明，所提出的时间触发VirtIO-net原型在网络负载条件下仍能为关键流量提供可预测的端到端延迟性能，验证了该方案在混合关键性虚拟化系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将时间触发机制与VirtIO虚拟网络相结合，可以在不依赖静态分区的情况下为混合关键性应用提供端到端时序保证。该方案成功将IEEE 802.1Qbv时间敏感网络标准扩展至虚拟化层，为未来安全关键型虚拟化系统的设计提供了可行的网络架构和调度方法。

Abstract: Virtualization enables mixed-criticality applications to run on shared hardware by isolating them within separate virtual machines (VMs). However, achieving end-to-end timing guarantees for critical tasks hosted on virtual CPUs (VCPUs) without relying on static partitioning is hard. A possible solution is a time-triggered hypervisor that dispatches VMs' VCPUs to processor cores according to a predetermined global schedule. A global schedule includes the computation, the network, and the virtualization layer to provide end-to-end timing guarantees across hosts and VMs during design time. In this work, we propose a VirtIO-based virtual network that realizes IEEE 802.1Qbv quasi-compliant inter-VM communication, providing predictable timing for critical messages exchanged between time-triggered VMs. We present our VirtIO-based virtual network architecture and derive network-level scheduling constraints that emerge from the properties of the proposed solution. We implement the proposed design in the ACRN hypervisor and evaluate the end-to-end latency of critical traffic in a loaded network, demonstrating that our time-triggered VirtIO-net prototype provides predictable end-to-end latency under load.

</details>


### [79] [Shortcut in Generalization: Rethinking Evaluation Protocol for Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17961)
*Qiming Yu,Chungang Lin,Zhengyi Ma,Zijia Song,Yuliang Lu,Guozheng Yang,Dongzhen Qi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 加密流量分类的评估协议存在捷径偏差：源IP重叠可使准确率虚高32-33个百分点，训练-测试处理不一致导致SOTA模型性能损失11-13个百分点；呼吁建立更严格的评估标准以确保泛化性能的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密流量分类通常在各种实验协议下进行评估，但报告的性能可能受到评估协议中未控制的捷径因素影响（如捷径特征和跨分区共享），而非反映可泛化的流量模式；此外，仅测试阶段的特征转换会进一步引入训练-测试不匹配问题。

Method: 系统研究了当前评估协议如何受捷径相关因素影响，包括字段级捷径、跨分区共享和训练-测试处理一致性。具体方法包括：比较特征重要性、重新训练的特征移除和仅字段分割敏感性；变化源IP重叠度和每流数据包数；比较仅测试阶段的IP转换与匹配的训练-测试转换。在三个公开数据集上进行实验。

Result: 现有方法的性能受到跨分区共享和训练-测试转换一致性的影响：(1) 源IP重叠从0%增加到100%时，标识符掩码前准确率提升约32-33个百分点，掩码后仅提升约8个百分点；(2) 对于两个最先进的预训练模型（YaTC和NetMamba），仅测试阶段IP置零造成的11-13个百分点损失，当训练测试应用相同转换时几乎完全恢复。

Conclusion: 当前加密流量分类的评估协议存在严重的捷径偏差问题，跨分区共享（尤其是源IP重叠）和训练-测试处理不一致会显著虚高模型性能。为确保评估结果反映真实的泛化能力，需要严格控制这些协议因素，包括消除跨分区样本关联和保持训练-测试阶段处理的一致性。

Abstract: Encrypted traffic classification is commonly evaluated under a range of experimental protocols, with classification performance reported as the primary measure. However, the reported performance may be influenced by shortcuts left uncontrolled in the evaluation protocol, such as shortcut features and cross-partition sharing, rather than reflect generalizable traffic patterns. And test-only feature transformations may further introduce train-test mismatch. In this paper, we study how current evaluation protocols are affected by shortcut-related factors, including field-level shortcuts, cross-partition sharing, and train-test processing. We compare feature importance, retrained feature removal, and field-only split sensitivity. We also vary Source-IP overlap and packets per flow, compare test-only IP transformations with matched train-test transformations. Experiments on three public datasets show that the performance of existing methods are affected by both cross-partition sharing and the consistency of train-test transformations. Specifically, increasing Source-IP overlap from 0% to 100% raises accuracy by about 32-33 points before identifier masking, compared with only about 8 points after masking. For two state-of-the-art pretrained models (i.e., YaTC and NetMamba), the 11-13 points loss caused by test-only IP zeroing is almost fully recovered when the same transformation is applied during both training and testing.

</details>


### [80] [CALM-5G: Event-Level Conformal Risk Control for Predictive Service-Assurance Alarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17962)
*Ömer Faruk İşcanlı,Yağmur Sabucu Sandal*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CALM-5G是一种可部署于任意预测模型之上的服务保障层，能够将KPI预测转换为具有分布无关、有限样本保证的告警，通过跨验证校准和在线校准在四个真实5G数据集上实现了告警时间显著降低（56.3%-89.7%），同时保证误报率可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性服务保障将关键性能指标（KPI）的预测转换为告警，但这一转换过程决定了保障功能是否可实际部署。现有方法缺乏严格的统计保证，无法确保告警的可靠性和可控性，特别是在5G网络的复杂环境下需要分布无关、有限样本保证的告警机制。

Method: 提出CALM-5G保障层，其核心方法包括：（1）在任意预测骨干网络之上运行，对服务级违规事件进行校准；（2）直接对违规起始（violation onset）进行评分；（3）通过样本外交叉验证选择最优校准方案；（4）联合认证告警占空比（duty cycle）和漏报率（miss rate）；（5）支持在线校准以应对标签漂移（label shift）；（6）考虑实体内部依赖性对校准单元的影响。

Result: 在四个独立数据集（生产蜂窝网络、商用5G trace、开放无线接入网测试平台、5G独立测试平台）上的实验结果表明：（1）基于回归分数的风险控制获得0.24至0.91的告警占空比；（2）起始目标定位结合交叉验证校准在三个数据集上将告警时间降低56.3%至89.7%，第四个数据集无显著变化；（3）实际漏报率在三个数据集上保持在两倍标准误差容限内，第四个数据集保持在一倍标准误差内；（4）标签漂移下，批量池化校准在生产数据集上结构性超出容限10.9个标准误差，而在线校准恢复合规性；（5）校准方案无法在其拟合阈值的分割上进行排序；（6）在实体内部依赖性留有足够校准单元时，漏报率和占空比可联合认证。

Conclusion: CALM-5G通过分布无关的有限样本保证机制，实现了预测性服务保障的可部署性。关键发现包括：跨验证校准显著优化告警效率，在线校准对标签漂移具有鲁棒性，校准方案的选择必须在样本外进行，且联合认证框架在考虑数据依赖性条件下有效。该方法为5G网络运维提供了理论保证与实用性能兼具的告警解决方案。

Abstract: Predictive service assurance converts forecasts of key performance indicators into alarms, and that conversion decides whether the assurance function is deployable. We present CALM-5G, an assurance layer that operates above any forecasting backbone and issues alarms with distribution-free, finite-sample guarantees. It calibrates on service-level violation events, scores violation onset directly, selects its calibration scheme out of sample, and reports the alarm duty cycle with every certified miss rate. Four corpora are evaluated separately, namely a production cellular network, commercial 5G traces, an open radio access network testbed and our 5G standalone testbed. Risk control on the regression-derived score yields duty cycles of 0.24 to 0.91. Onset targeting with cross-validated calibration reduces time-in-alarm by 56.3% to 89.7% on three corpora, with no significant change on the fourth. The realised miss rate stays within a two-standard-error tolerance on three corpora and within one standard error of it on the fourth. Under label shift, pooled batch calibration exceeds that tolerance structurally, by 10.9 standard errors on the production corpus. Online calibration restores compliance. Calibration schemes cannot be ranked on the split that fits their thresholds. Miss rate and duty cycle are certified jointly where within-entity dependence leaves sufficient calibration units.

</details>


### [81] [NetCoFi: A Budget-Aware Coarse-Fine Dual-Branch Model for Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17978)
*Zhengyi Ma,Qiming Yu,Chungang Lin,Wentao Cui,Zijia Song,Guozheng Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NetCoFi是一种预算感知的加密流量分类器，通过粗粒度和细粒度计算分配，在有限资源下实现准确率与效率的最佳权衡，在低预算场景下Macro-F1平均提升7.86个百分点，吞吐量较NetMamba提升1.64倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密流量分类对网络管理和安全至关重要，但实际部署需要在分类准确率和推理成本之间取得平衡。现有模型通常处理固定流窗口，训练后难以控制计算量，这在资源受限环境下限制了准确率-效率的权衡空间。

Method: 提出NetCoFi，一种预算感知分类器，采用双粒度计算分配机制：（1）利用字节表示和可观测包元数据，选择token邻域进行细粒度编码；（2）将完整窗口压缩为互补的粗粒度上下文；（3）多预算训练使同一模型可在不同路由预算下运行而无需重新训练。

Result: 在六个公开数据集上，NetCoFi从零训练达到最优分类性能；在低预算设置下，相比同深度静态基线，在三个数据集上平均Macro-F1提升7.86个百分点；在DPDK-GPU流量处理流水线中，平均批次吞吐量从26.42 Mb/s提升至43.42 Mb/s，较NetMamba提升1.64倍。

Conclusion: NetCoFi通过粗-细粒度自适应计算分配和多预算训练机制，有效解决了加密流量分类中准确率与效率的权衡问题，在保持高准确率的同时显著提升了低预算场景下的推理效率，具有广泛的实际部署价值。

Abstract: Encrypted traffic classification is critical for network management and security, but practical deployment must balance classification accuracy and inference cost. Existing models typically process fixed flow windows with limited control over computation after training, which can limit the accuracy-efficiency trade-off under constrained resources. To address this problem, we present NetCoFi, a budget-aware classifier that allocates computation at coarse and fine granularities. Using byte representations and observable packet metadata, NetCoFi selects token neighborhoods for fine-grained encoding while compressing the full window into complementary coarse context. Multi-budget training enables the same model to operate at different routing budgets without retraining. Evaluation on six public datasets shows that NetCoFi achieves state-of-the-art classification performance when trained from scratch. Under low-budget settings, NetCoFi improves average Macro-F1 by 7.86 percentage points across three datasets, compared with a same-depth static baseline. In a DPDK-GPU traffic processing pipeline, it increases mean batch throughput from 26.42 to 43.42 Mb/s, a 1.64x improvement over NetMamba.

</details>


### [82] [Developments in Building Towards the Indian SKA Regional Centre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18091)
*Rohit Sharma,Yogesh Wadadekar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文分析了SKA区域中心网络（SRCNet）向v0.2版本演进的架构需求，提出了原型基础设施的建设策略，并针对主次节点开展原型开发，为印度SKA区域中心（INSRC）的完全运行提供了工程路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平方公里阵列射电望远镜（SKA）观测站（SKAO）产生的数据产品需要全球化的基础设施进行存档、分发和处理，因此需要构建SKA区域中心网络（SRCNet）。随着架构从早期版本向v0.2演进，亟需明确计算、编排、多级存储等方面的需求，并建立原型基础设施以验证可行性。

Method: 本文分析了SRCNet架构向v0.2版本过渡的演进过程，重点研究了计算、编排和多级存储需求；详细阐述了原型SRC（proto-SRC）基础设施的建设策略；并针对主要节点和次要节点开展了原型开发工作，具体描述了次要节点的计算与存储需求。

Result: 明确了SRCNet v0.2版本的架构要求，建立了从原型阶段向完全运行的印度SKA区域中心（INSRC）演进的技术路径；完成了主要节点和次要节点的原型开发规划，为次要节点的计算和存储资源配置提供了量化依据。

Conclusion: SRCNet v0.2架构的演进为SKAO数据产品的全球化管理提供了可扩展的技术框架。通过proto-SRC基础设施的建设和节点原型开发，验证了区域中心网络的技术可行性，并为印度SKA区域中心（INSRC）的全面运行奠定了工程基础。

Abstract: The SKA Regional Centre Network (SRCNet) is a federated global network designed to archive, distribute, and process SKAO data products. This paper examines the evolving SRCNet architecture-focusing on the transition to version 0.2 (v0.2) requirements, including computation, orchestration, and multi-tiered storage-and details the strategy for establishing its proto-SRC infrastructure. We outline the prototype developments for the major and minor nodes in the context of v0.2.
